> Cikakken bayani na kowane environment variable da OmniRoute ke ganewa.
> Don samfurin farawa cikin sauri, duba [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Dole ne kowane variable da aka rubuta a nan ya bayyana a cikin `.env.example`, kuma
> kowane variable da ke cikin `.env.example` dole ne ya bayyana a nan. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> yana tabbatar da wannan yayin commit da kuma a cikin CI. Don tsallake wani variable da gangan, ƙara shi zuwa
> allowlist da ke cikin `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Sirrukan da Ake Buƙata

**Dole ne** a saita waɗannan kafin fara gudanarwa na farko. Idan babu su, application ɗin zai ƙi farawa ko kuma ya yi aiki da tsoffin saitunan da ba su da tsaro.

| Sauyi                        | Wajibi              | Tsoho                         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------- | ----------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Eh**              | _(babu)_                      | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Yana sanya hannu/tabbatar da duk kukis na zaman dashboard (JWT). Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Eh**              | _(babu)_                      | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                   | Maɓallin ɓoyewar AES don ƙimomin maɓallan API da aka adana a SQLite. Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | **Eh**              | `CHANGEME`                    | Rubutun bootstrap                                         | Yana saita kalmar sirrin farko ta dashboard na mai gudanarwa (ta dace da tsohuwar ƙimar `.env.example` — an bar ta a bayyane cewa ba ta da tsaro domin tilasta canjawa). **Canza kafin amfani na farko.** Bayan shiga, canza ta ta Dashboard → Settings → Security.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Eh** (production) | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Sirrin haɗin gwiwa don gadar WebSocket ta Codex Responses ta ciki. Yana tantance buƙatun gada tsakanin mai isar da WS na Electron/browser da OmniRoute. ⚠️ **ANA BUKATA a production — idan ba a saita shi ba, za a ƙi duk buƙatun gadar WS.** Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | A'a                 | _(git SHA)_                   | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Bayyanannen id na karya-cache na service-worker don harsashin PWA (#11779); shi ne na farko a jerin warwarewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | A'a                 | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Na biyu a jerin — masu gina PaaS (misali irin na Heroku) ne ke saita shi a matsayin commit da aka tura.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | A'a                 | _(an samo)_                   | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Ƙimar jama'a ta lokacin ginawa da client ke amfani da ita don yin rajistar `/sw.js?v=…`; ana samo ta daga biyun da ke sama, sannan git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | A'a (ta atomatik)   | _(ta atomatik a kowane boot)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Sirri na kowane process da ke tabbatar da cewa hatimin peer-IP da aka amince da shi ya fito ne daga HTTP server na OmniRoute (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware na authz yana amincewa da kasancewar buƙata ta gida (toshewar loopback/LAN ga routes na LOCAL_ONLY) ne kawai idan hatimin yana ɗauke da wannan token. Ana ƙirƙira shi ta atomatik a kowane boot — bar shi ba a saita ba; saita shi dindindin kawai don tsarin multi-process da dole su raba hatimin. |

### Umarnin Ƙirƙirawa

```bash
# Ƙirƙiri duk sirrin guda huɗu a lokaci guda:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Kada ka taɓa commit na fayilolin `.env` masu ɗauke da sirri na ainihi zuwa version control. `.gitignore` ya riga ya ware `.env`, amma ka tabbatar kafin pushing.

---

## 2. Ma’ajiya da Rumbun Bayanai

OmniRoute yana amfani da **SQLite** (ta hanyar `better-sqlite3`) don duk wani adana bayanai na dindindin. Waɗannan masu canjin suna sarrafa wurin bayanai, ɓoyewa, da zagayowar rayuwa.

| Mai Canji                                  | Tsoho                         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Babban kundin adireshi na SQLite DB, kwafin ajiya, da fayilolin bayanai. Sauya shi don kundin Docker ko hanyoyi na musamman.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Hanyar kaucewa kariyar DATA_DIR ta gwaji/kimantawa (#10428). Ana karkatar da gwaje-gwaje da binciken eval/print na Node (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, har da siffofin `--eval=`/`--print=`) waɗanda ba su da `DATA_DIR` zuwa kundin wucin-gadi da za a yar, don kada su iya buɗe ainihin rumbun bayanan mai gudanarwa; saita zuwa `1` don sake amincewa da amfani da ainihin kundin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA na kayan da ke gudana. Ana buga shi ta `npm run build:release`; ana iya shigar da shi cikin kwantenonin da ake tura su ba tare da alamar `dist/BUILD_SHA` ba. Ana nuna shi a matsayin `system.buildSha` a `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref ɗin da ƙofar tabbatar da asalin kayan kunshewa ke gwada build SHA da shi (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Saita zuwa `1` don ba da damar kunshe build wanda SHA ɗinsa ba ya kan layin fitarwa, tare da yin rikodinsa a matsayin canary na ganganci maimakon sa ƙofar ta gaza (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | Maɓallin API don gwajin smoke na tura canary, wanda ake aikawa a matsayin `Authorization: Bearer` a `/v1/chat/completions`. Rubutun tura manhaja kaɗai ne ke amfani da shi (#10429), uwar garken ba ta taɓa amfani da shi. Ba shi da alaƙa da masu canjin `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` na tsarin gwajin smoke na CLI da ake kunna bisa zaɓi (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` a cikin `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — duba [Haɗe-haɗen CLI → Gwajin smoke na ainihi](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Alamar matakin build (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` ne ke saita ta zuwa `1`, kuma kowane ma’aikacin build da aka ƙaddamar yana gadonta domin matakin DB ya mayar da stub mara aiki maimakon loda native addon na better-sqlite3 (wanda ke dakatar da ma’aikacin yayin fita). Ba a taɓa saita ta ga uwar garken da ke gudana ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake binciken prebuild na native-dependency na zaɓi ga bishiyoyin vendored na musamman. Wannan ba ya sa dependency da babu ya zama abin ginawa; yi amfani da shi kawai idan ana samar da native dependencies ta wata hanya dabam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Madadin fallback** na `DATA_DIR`, wanda ake dubawa kawai idan ba a saita `DATA_DIR` ba. Ana amfani da shi don gano ma’ajiyar zaman thread-sticky ta PromptQL executor da ke kan faifai (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); idan babu ɗaya daga cikin masu canjin da aka saita, ma’ajiyar za ta kasance cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai (ba za ta dawwama bayan sake farawa ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Kundin adireshin da **runtime plugin scanner** ke karantawa — kuma tushen da plugin manager ke girkawa a ciki — yana maye gurbin tsohon wurin da aka samo daga home (#11827). Nuna shi zuwa bishiyar plugin da aka bind-mount a Docker/K8s maimakon matsar da HOME kawai don sauya wurin hanyar dubawa (HOME yana sarrafa duk sauran halayen da suka danganci home ma). Ba a saita ba = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ko `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` idan tsarin bai fitar da home ko kaɗan ba — wato rashin ganowa a ɓoye da wannan mai canjin ke kawarwa. Ana rubuta kundin adireshin da aka warware sau ɗaya yayin farawa a matsayin `scanner.dir_resolved`, tare da shigarwar da ta yi nasara. Na ɓangaren uwar garke kawai: plugins na umarnin CLI suna ci gaba da amfani da nasu `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` (sashe na 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(babu komai = an kashe)_     | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Maɓallin AES don cikakken ɓoye bayanan SQLite yayin da suke a ajiye. Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Alamar sigar maɓallin ɓoyewa. Ƙara ta yayin sauya maɓalli domin tallafa wa warware ɓoyewar tsofaffin kwafin ajiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Idan `true`, yana tsallake kwafin ajiyar fayil ɗin SQLite na yau da kullum/kafin rubutu (adana/share farashin models.dev, rubuta saituna). Kwafin ajiya na hannu da na kafin maidowa har yanzu suna gudana. **Ba ya** kashe hoton kariya mai ɗorewa na tilas na mai gudanar da ƙaura ko kariyar ƙaura mai yawa ga DB mai ɗorewa da yake akwai. Ana iyakance kwafin ajiyar da ba na hannu ba zuwa sau ɗaya mafi yawa a kowane minti 60. **Settings → Storage** na dashboard na iya kashe kwafin ajiya na yau da kullum da kansa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Tsohon suna na madadin** don `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Ana karɓarsa a matsayin madadin idan babban canjin ba ya nan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Tsohon suna na madadin** (nau'in da aka sauya zuwa Base64) da ake karɓa a matsayin madadin. Ana warware shi ta atomatik kafin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Sauya tazarar binciken lafiyar SQLite na lokaci-lokaci (ms). Idan ba a saita ba, ana samo ƙimomin tsoho daga `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(an cire)_                   | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **An cire.** `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` mai gudana lokaci-lokaci na iya lalata rabaffen taswirar wal-index kuma ya sa tsarin ya faɗi da SIGBUS (#13973), saboda haka babu mai tsara lokacin kuma. Canjin ba ya aiki: ƙima mai kyau tana rubuta gargaɗin daina amfani sau ɗaya, yayin da `0` ko rashin saiti ba ya fitar da komai. Ana kula da WAL ta wuraren dubawa na PASSIVE (a ƙasa), kuma wurin dubawa na rufewa yana rage shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (5m)                 | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Sauya tazarar `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` mai yawan gudana (ms). Yana rage firamomin WAL da ke jira domin wuraren dubawa su kasance masu sauri kuma girman fayil ɗin WAL ya kasance cikin iyaka tsakanin rage-ragen lokacin rufewa. `0` yana kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Idan zagayen PASSIVE ya gano fayil ɗin WAL ya wuce wannan girman, gudanar da `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` domin WAL ya fara daga farko ba tare da sake rubuta wal-index da aka tsara ba. An cire wuraren dubawa masu yanayin ragewa yayin aiki (duba layin `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` domin fita daga tsarin bayan matsanancin matsin albarkatu ya ci gaba na tsawon `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, wanda zai ba mai sa ido (systemd `Restart=always`, dokar sake kunnawa ta Docker) damar dawo da tsari mai tsabta maimakon ci gaba da bayar da 503s har abada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (2m)                 | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tsawon lokacin da matsanancin matsin lamba dole ya ci gaba kafin fitowar sake kunna kai ta fara aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_        | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Takamaiman hanyar fayil (cikakkiya ko dangane da cwd) zuwa `sql-wasm.wasm` yayin amfani da adaftan madadin WASM na `sql.js`. Ana gano ta atomatik ta hanyar dogarorin fakiti da tsare-tsaren wurare masu yiwuwa idan ba a saita ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Yawan kwanakin da ake riƙe wuraren dubawa, fayilolin shigarwa/fitarwa/kuskure da aka ambata, da layin aikin Batch API da ya kai ƙarshe (an kammala/ya gaza/an soke/ya ƙare) ta hanyar sharewar atomatik kafin a goge su. Yana fara aiki ne kawai bayan an kunna `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; ya yi daidai da wa'adin riƙe sakamakon Batch API na OpenAI. Ba ya shafar hanyar `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` da mai gudanarwa ke ƙaddamarwa, wadda bisa tsari take ci gaba da aiki ba tare da wani sharadin shekaru ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Idan `true`, yana barin sharewar atomatik ta goge ayyukan Batch API da suka kai ƙarshe (da wuraren dubawarsu) bayan `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, sannan ta share abubuwan BLOB na fayilolin da aka loda bayan `expires_at` nasu. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa: kowace shigarwa da take akwai tana riƙe waɗannan bayanai daidai kamar da har sai mai gudanarwa ya zaɓi shiga. Haka kuma tutar fasali ce da za a iya gyarawa daga dashboard — duba `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Lokacin aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake duba lafiyar DB gaba ɗaya yayin farawa. Yana da amfani ga ayyuka masu ɗan gajeren lokaci da gwaje-gwajen haɗawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` don tilasta kunna madaukin duba lafiyar DB, ko da a yanayin da aka saba tsallake shi (misali, ayyuka masu ɗan gajeren lokaci).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake shirya native-runtime yayin `npm install`. Yana da amfani a shigarwar CI/headless inda aka riga aka gina sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_        | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Sauya kundin adireshin da mai gudanar da ƙaura ke bincikawa. Yana da amfani yayin tura ƙaura da aka haɗa cikin gini na musamman.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ƙarin kundin adireshin ƙaura a matsayin shigarwar `namespace=dir` da aka raba da alamar raba path ta dandamali (misali `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). Ana rubuta fayilolin da aka samu a wurin a matsayin `<namespace>-<number>`, don haka rarrabawar da ke ɗauke da ƙaurarta ba za ta taɓa cin karo da guraben lambobi na upstream ba. Shigarwa mara tsari, namespace mara inganci, ko kundin adireshi da babu shi zai haifar da kuskure yayin farawa maimakon tsallake schema a ɓoye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Iyakar kariya ga yawan ƙaura masu jiran aiki (#3416). Farawa zai tsaya idan adadin ƙaura masu jiran aiki a DB da ke akwai ya zarce wannan adadi (yana kariya daga goge teburin bin diddigi). Ƙara shi don maido da tsohon backup; saita zuwa `0` don kashe duba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Kundin adireshin aiki don matakin tantance fitarwa na `check:install-upgrade`. Yana buƙatar kusan 12 GB (bishiyoyin shigarwa guda biyu na kusan 3 GB kowanne tare da tarball), don haka kada ya gudana a ƙaramin tmpfs — a self-hosted runner ɗin, `/tmp` tmpfs ne mai goyon bayan RAM na 12 GB kuma matakin tantancewar ya cika shi, wanda ya sa aka datse package ɗin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(tsohon saiti a cikin code)_ | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Tazarar flush (ms) ga marubucin kashe kuɗi/farashi na batch. Ƙananan ƙimomi suna rage haɗa rubuce-rubuce; manyan ƙimomi suna rage cunkoson DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(tsohon saiti a cikin code)_ | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar kashe kuɗi da za a riƙe a buffer kafin tilasta flush. Ƙara shi a turawar da ke da babban QPS; rage shi idan iyakantaccen memory ya fi muhimmanci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Saita zuwa `"true"` don fitar da debug logs na `[ProxyFetch]` a path ɗin relay na Vercel. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa don kauce wa fallasa alamun routing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` don haɗa IP na client/egress da prefix na account a cikin cikakken layin process-log na `[ProxyEgress]`. Ana barinsa a KASHE ta tsohuwa don kada process log ya fallasa IP ko prefix na account.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Saita zuwa `1` don buga bayanan gano matsala na lokacin kowace request (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) daga umarnin quota na CLI zuwa stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(atomatik)_                  | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Takamaiman path da duba lafiyar container ke gwadawa. Idan ba a saita ba, probe ɗin yana samo shi daga `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; saita shi yana sake kunna deep monitoring endpoint.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa kowace ƙima marar fanko don fitar da bayanan gano matsala na `[omniroute completion]` daga paths na cache na kammalawar shell na CLI (karantawa/sabuntawa/rubutawa). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — gazawar waɗannan caches tana faruwa a ɓoye don kada cache da babu shi ko ya lalace ya taɓa hana tab-completion aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)              | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin tagar sake gwadawa ga kowane abu na batch (ms). Abubuwan da suka wuce wannan tsawon lokaci ana yi musu alamar gazawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Jinkirin asali (ms) don ƙarin jinkiri mai ninkawa yayin sake gwada abubuwan batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (awa 1)             | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Iyakar jinkiri (ms) don ƙarin jinkiri mai ninkawa tsakanin sake gwada abubuwan batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin adadin batch da ake sarrafawa a lokaci guda. Ƙara shi don haɓaka yawan aiki; bar shi ƙasa don guje wa ambaliyar kai iyakar ƙimar amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Kafin a canza wata ma'ajiyar bayanai mai ɗorewa da take akwai, mai gudanar da ƙaura yana wallafa cikakken
> hoton-ma'ajiya mai adireshin-abun-ciki a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Wallafawa na buƙatar tsarin fayil
> da ke goyon bayan hard links marasa-rubutawa-a-kan-wani a cikin tsarin fayil guda, tare da daidaita fayil zuwa ma'ajiya mai ɗorewa. Masu masaukin POSIX kuma
> suna buƙatar daidaita kundin adireshi; a Windows, Node na iya ƙin hannayen kundin adireshi, don haka OmniRoute yana kammala rubuta
> fayil ɗin da aka wallafa kuma yana ɗaukar daidaita shigarwar kundin adireshi a matsayin ƙoƙari gwargwadon iko.
> Idan `DATA_DIR` da aka ɗora ba zai iya samar da waɗannan tabbacin ba, farawa zai tsaya cikin aminci kafin aiwatar
> da ƙaura. Matsar da `DATA_DIR` zuwa volume mai waɗannan dabaru; kada a yi amfani da
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` don tsallake amincin ƙaura.

### Yanayin Amfani

| Yanayi                          | Tsarawa                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ci gaban cikin gida**         | Bar duk tsoffin saituna. DB tana a `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.                         |
| **Docker**                      | `DATA_DIR=/data` + ɗora volume a `/data`.                                               |
| **Rufaffen bayanai a ma'ajiya** | Saita `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + adana kwafin ajiyar maɓallin! Rasa shi = rasa bayanai. |
| **CI/Gwaji**                    | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — na wucin gadi ne, ba a buƙatar ɓoyewa.                 |

---

## 3. Hanyar Sadarwa & Tashoshin Haɗi

| Sauyawa                                     | Tsoho                                                      | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                                    | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Babban tashar haɗi don Dashboard UI da maɓuɓɓugan API (yanayin tashar haɗi guda ɗaya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(babu komai = tushe)_                                     | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Ƙaramin hanyar URL don gabatar da OmniRoute a bayan reverse proxy (yana saita `basePath` na Next.js; turawa na tantancewa suna la'akari da basePath). Misali `/omniroute`. A Docker, ana haɗa ƙimar yayin `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); hotunan tushe da aka riga aka gina za su iya amfani da wata ƙimar lokacin aiki sau ɗaya a farkon container kafin Next.js ya fara. Saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa public origin wanda ya haɗa da wannan ƙaramar hanyar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(babu komai = tushe)_                                     | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Kwafin `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` da browser ke iya gani, wanda ake saka shi kai tsaye a lokacin gini domin nunin endpoint na dashboard ya nuna `https://host/omniroute/v1` maimakon `https://host/v1`. Idan ba a saita shi ba, yana komawa ga `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`. Sake ginawa bayan canza shi (`basePath` na Next ana ƙayyade shi ne lokacin gini).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(ba a saita ba = ba za a taɓa iya sakawa cikin frame ba)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Zaɓin yarda da saka shafukan HTML cikin iframe. Idan ba a saita ba, kowace route tana zuwa da `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Saita zuwa `vscode` don gabatar da shafukan (dashboard, login, docs, landing) tare da `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` kuma ba tare da `X-Frame-Options` ba, domin VS Code Simple Browser ya iya nuna su (yanayin `dashboardOpen: "editor"` na OmniCopilot). Fuskar API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, laƙabban matakin tushe) tana riƙe tsauraran headers ko ta yaya. `vscode` kaɗai ake ganewa — `1`/`true` ba sa kunna shi. Na lokacin gini: sake ginawa bayan canzawa (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` don hotuna; saita shi a kan shigarwar da aka riga aka gina ba ya da tasiri). |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                          | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita, yana gabatar da proxy API na `/v1/*` a wannan tashar haɗi ta daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                                  | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adireshin da tashar API za ta ɗaure gare shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                          | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita, yana gabatar da Dashboard UI a wannan tashar haɗi ta daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(a kashe)_                                            | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Yana iyakance adadin haɗin tattaunawa masu gudana lokaci guda; buƙatun da suka wuce iyakar suna samun `503` tare da `Retry-After`. Cikakken lamba mai kyau tana kunna kariyar; rashin saiti/`0` yana kashe ta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                          | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | ID mai ɗorewa kuma na musamman ga wannan gateway yayin haɗa instances na OmniRoute a jere. Yana kunna binciken inbound peer-loop. Haruffan da aka yarda da su: haruffa, lambobi, `.`, `_`, `:`, da `-`; iyakar haruffa 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                          | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | Jerin tushen URLs na OmniRoute da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za su iya karɓar `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. URLs na upstream da aka amince da su a sarari kaɗai ne ke karɓar metadata na peer; duk sauran masu samarwa ba a taɓa su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                                        | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Matsakaicin adadin instans na OmniRoute da aka ziyarta a baya waɗanda ake karɓa a kan buƙata mai sarƙa (`1`-`32`). Maimaita instans ko ƙarewar kasafin yunƙuri yana dawo da HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                                                    | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port ɗin da aka wallafa a ɓangaren host don Dashboard a yanayin samarwa na Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                                                    | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port ɗin da aka wallafa a ɓangaren host don API a yanayin samarwa na Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                          | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Yana da fifiko a kan `PORT` lokacin aiki a cikin Electron ko wasu wrappers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                                    | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Port don uwar garken sa ido kai-tsaye na WebSocket na ainihin lokaci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                                | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Adireshin bind don uwar garken WebSocket kai-tsaye. Saita zuwa `0.0.0.0` don fallasa shi a LAN (sannan kuma saita `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                                          | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Ƙarin origins da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka ba su izinin buɗe WebSocket kai-tsaye. An riga an ba da izini ga origins na dashboard na loopback ta tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                          | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Ƙarin hostnames da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka ba su izini don origins na WebSocket kai-tsaye. Ba kamar `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (cikakkun URLs na origin) ba, yana daidaita ɓangaren host kawai — yana da amfani ga saitunan LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                          | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL na jama'a don WebSocket na dashboard kai-tsaye (a ɓangaren browser). Saita shi lokacin sanya reverse proxy ko Cloudflare Tunnel a gaban uwar garken WS (misali `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); browser zai haɗa a can maimakon `ws://hostname:20132`. Haka kuma ana amfani da ɓangaren pathname a matsayin hanyar upgrade ta WebSocket (tsoho: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                                     | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` da `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Saita zuwa `0` ko `false` don kashe uwar garken WebSocket na ainihin lokaci (an kunna shi ta tsoho, kuma an ɗaure shi da loopback). Maɓallin CI/harness ne da ke kashe standalone helper script na WebSocket kai-tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                                       | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Iyakar ƙimar relay ta kowane (token, IP), buƙatu/minti. A cikin memory, ga kowane instans. `0` ko ƙasa da sifili yana kashe ƙofar iyakar IP-dimension (iyakar DB ta kowane token har yanzu tana aiki).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                                               | Next.js core                                                             | Yana sarrafa yawan bayanan log, caching, bayyana cikakkun bayanan kuskure, da ingantawar Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | `1` (Turbopack — tsohon saitin lamba)                      | `package.json` / Next.js 16                                              | Turbopack shi ne bundler na asali don `npm run dev` da `npm run build` (ginawa da sauri sau 2-3, bisa gwajin ma'auni). Saita zuwa `0` don komawa webpack a Windows, idan an ci karo da rashin jituwa ta native binding / bundler, **ko a na'urori masu ƙarancin RAM** — an san cewa ginin production na Turbopack a wannan layin sigar Next.js (16.2.x) yana kaiwa kololuwa mafi girma sosai wajen amfani da ƙwaƙwalwa fiye da webpack a manyan module graphs (gyaran korar bayanan ƙwaƙwalwa na Turbopack a Next 16.3 bai zama stable ba tukuna); komawa webpack yana kaiwa kololuwa mafi ƙasa sosai. Duba #6409.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                          | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake binciken lafiyar mutuncin SQLite yayin farawa. Yana da amfani don saurin farawa a manyan ma'ajin bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                          | systemd (sd_notify protocol)                                             | systemd ne ke saita shi lokacin da tsari ke gudana ƙarƙashin service unit mai haɗin sd_notify; OmniRoute yana karanta shi (duba `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) don aika sanarwar READY/WATCHDOG. Kada mai amfani ya taɓa saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                          | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Saita zuwa `1` don kashe systemd sd_notify (Type=notify / WatchdogSec=) ko da ana gudana ƙarƙashin systemd unit. Notifier ba ya yin komai a wajen systemd ko ta yaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `300000`                                                   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Tazarar lokaci (ms) don mai tsara binciken lafiyar credential da ke gudana a bango. Mafi ƙanƙanta: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                                                   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) don matsayin lafiyar credential da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                                                    | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` ko `true` don kashe gwajin haɗin provider na lokaci-lokaci da ke gudana a bango. Ana cire search providers (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS a cikin `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, misali `tavily-search`) koyaushe daga binciken — "validation" ɗinsu ainihin query ce ta upstream mai kuɗi, saboda haka ba a taɓa yi musu health check bisa lokaci ba (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                                                  | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Adireshin bind na Next.js dev/start server.
