Ana iya maye gurbinsa don relays / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Lokacin turawa a bayan reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa tsayayyen URL ɗinka na jama'a (misali, `https://omniroute.example.com`) idan dole ne callbacks na OAuth ko hanyoyin haɗin jama'a da aka ƙirƙira su yi amfani da wannan hostname. Idan babu wannan, callbacks na OAuth na iya gaza saboda redirect_uri ba zai dace ba, kuma hanyoyin haɗin jama'a da aka ƙirƙira za su iya nuni zuwa asalin container na ciki.
>
> Riƙe `BASE_URL` a matsayin URL na loopback/container na ciki don ayyukan server-to-server. Kada a yi amfani da `Origin` na burauza ko hostname na jama'a don internal self-fetches masu ɗauke da bayanan shaidar shiga.
>
> Rubuce-rubucen dashboard da aka tantance ba sa buƙatar tsayayyen base URL na jama'a: dashboard yana aika buƙatun same-origin marasa aminci tare da token na CSRF da aka ɗaure da session. Har yanzu OmniRoute yana tattara ingantawar public-origin a wuri guda don haɗin gwiwar burauza da ba na dashboard ba: an fara amincewa da env vars na URL na jama'a da aka fayyace; ana yin watsi da ɗanyen headers na `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` sai dai idan an kunna `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` kuma an yi wa proxy peer na kai tsaye alamar token a matsayin amintacce. Kada a yi amfani da saitunan CORS don gyara buƙatun dashboard na same-origin; CORS na abokan hulɗar burauza na cross-origin ne kawai.

---

## 8. Wakilin Hanyar Fita

Tura kiran masu samar da LLM na sama ta hanyar wakilin HTTP ko SOCKS5 domin sarrafa zirga-zirgar fita, zaɓar hanya bisa yanki, ko ɓoye adireshin IP.

| Sauyi                                    | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`            | `open-sse/executors`                         | Kunna wakilin SOCKS5 don kiraye-kirayen sama. Kashe shi da `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`            | Gefen abokin ciniki                          | Fahimtar samuwar SOCKS5 a gefen abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `false`           | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`           | Tutar fasalin da ake kunna bisa zaɓi (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); saitin maye gurbi na DB a dashboard ne ke rinjaya). Tarin wakilai da juyawar kowane asusu za su daina sake ba da wani mamba da ya gaza kwanan nan (an ƙi gwajin TCP, ko an samu 429 ta cikinsa) na wani lokaci da ke ninkuwa duk lokacin da gazawar ta maimaitu, har zuwa iyaka. `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana kunna shi.                      |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                             | Wakilin HTTP don kiraye-kirayen sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                             | Wakilin HTTPS don kiraye-kirayen sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                             | Wakili na gama-gari (yana goyon bayan `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                 | Yana ƙayyade manufa guda ta URL don gano IP da binciken zirga-zirgar fita ta wakili ke amfani da ita. Idan ba a saita ba, binciken yana gwada `api64.ipify.org` sannan `api4.ipify.org` don kada a ba da rahoton hanyoyin da ke amfani da IPv4 kawai a matsayin matattu (#9694).                                                                                                                                                |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                             | Sunayen runduna/adiresoshin IP da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za a kewaye wakilin dominsu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`              | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`          | Matsakaicin soket-soket masu aiki lokaci guda ga kowane mai rarraba wakilin HTTP/SOCKS da aka adana. Rafukan SSE masu daɗewa kamar Codex `/v1/responses` suna buƙatar haɗi fiye da ɗaya lokacin da buƙatu da yawa suke amfani da wakilin matakin asusu ɗaya. Ana iyakance ƙimomin da suka wuce `256`.                                                                                                                           |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`           | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts` | Iyakar lokacin musayar farko ta SOCKS5 (haɗawa) a ms. Ƙara shi idan buƙatu da yawa suna bugun rundunar ƙofar sadarwar gida guda ɗaya lokaci guda (misali, buƙatu 100 a lokaci ɗaya) — a ƙarƙashin tarin da ya cika, ainihin musayar farko na iya wuce 10s ko da yake ana iya isa ga wakilin, wanda in ba haka ba zai bayyana a matsayin kuskuren ƙarya na `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable`. An iyakance shi zuwa `120000`. |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`           | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`            | Lokacin da yake `false` (tsoho), buƙatar da wakilin da aka sanya mata ya kasa warwarewa za a **ƙi ta (fail-closed)** maimakon komawa ga haɗin kai tsaye — wannan yana hana fallasa ainihin IP. Saita `true` don dawo da tsohon tsarin komawa DIRECT.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`           | `open-sse/executors`                         | Yi kwaikwayon sawun yatsan TLS ta amfani da wreq-js (yana kwaikwayon Chrome 124). Yana magance toshewar JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`               | Jerin masu samarwa da aka amince da su, wanda aka raba da waƙafi, don sabon tsarin tura TLS ta wakili (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). TLS kai tsaye yana ci gaba da tsohon halinsa idan ba a saita shi ba; waɗannan masu samarwa kaɗai ne ake tura su ta gadar sawun yatsa ta Chrome-124.                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`           | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts` | Ba mahallin burauzar Playwright na Claude Turnstile damar yin watsi da kurakuran takardar shaidar HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Yanayi

| Yanayi                                                           | Saitin                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 ta cikin ramin SSH**                                    | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                                                    |
| **Wakilin HTTP na kamfani**                                      | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com`                          |
| **Kare sawun yatsa**                                             | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — yana buƙatar `wreq-js` (an haɗa shi)                                                                               |
| **Ana sarrafa zirga-zirgar fita / babu damar kai tsaye**         | Bar `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (tsoho). Buƙatu za su gaza gaba ɗaya idan wakilin ba ya samuwa maimakon fallasa zirga-zirga ta hanyar haɗin kai tsaye. |
| **Tsohon tsari / haɓakawa — ba da damar komawa haɗin kai tsaye** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Yana maido da halin kafin ƙarfafa tsaro: ana amfani da haɗin kai tsaye idan gano wakili ya gaza.                           |

> **Lura (tsallake tantancewar NVIDIA — #3226):** Wurin ƙarshen tantance maɓallin API na NVIDIA
> yana maƙalewa idan aka tura shi ta hanyar wakili na gaba ɗaya/fetch da aka yi wa facin TLS (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` da gangan yana tsallake
> facin wakili don wannan kiran tantancewa guda ɗaya ta amfani da `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Wannan keɓantawa ce da aka rubuta kuma aka iyakance — **ba ya** shafar zirga-zirgar fita ta taɗi/amfani.
> An ɗaure iyakar tsallakewar ta hanyar `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts`.

---

## 9. Haɗa Kayan Aikin CLI

Yana sarrafa yadda OmniRoute ke ganowa da ƙaddamar da sidecars na CLI (Claude Code, Codex, da sauransu).

| Maɓalli                           | Tsoho                            | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = bincika PATH na tsarin; `manual` = yi amfani da takamaiman hanyoyi kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ƙarin shigarwar PATH don gano fayilolin binary na CLI (waɗanda aka raba da alamar colon).                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Sauya kundin adireshin gida don karanta saitunan CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Dole ne ya zama cikakkiyar hanya kuma ya kasance cikin gidan tsarin aiki — **ko kuma**, a cikin container, hanya da aka bind-mount (haka `/host-home` yake aiki). Duk wani abu dabam zai koma ga kundin adireshin gida. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ba OmniRoute damar rubuta fayilolin saitin CLI (sabunta token, bayanan zaman aiki). | `ZCODE_RPC_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `30000`                          | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Iyakar lokacin RPC na kowace buƙata (ms) don kiran app-server na ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ZCODE_TURN_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `120000`                         | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Matsakaicin tsawon lokaci (ms) na zagayen ZCode guda kafin mai sa ido ya katse shi saboda ƙarewar lokaci.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ZCODE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`          | `250`                            | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Tazarar bincike (ms) don kammala zagayen ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `HERMES_HOME`                     | `~/.hermes`                      | `src/lib/cli-helper/config-generator/hermesHome.ts` | Kundin gida na Hermes Agent inda OmniRoute ke karantawa/rubuta saitunan Hermes CLI. Ya yi daidai da env var da mai girka Hermes PowerShell ke saitawa a Windows (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes`).                                                                                                              |

### Daidaita Bayanan Martabar CLI Ta Atomatik

Waɗannan tutocin fasali na zaɓi ne kuma a kashe suke ta tsohuwa. Hakanan ana iya kunna su ko kashe su daga
dashboard na CLI Code.

| Canji                                 | Tsoho   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                  | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin bayanan martaba na `~/.codex/*.config.toml` ta atomatik daga kundin kai-tsaye. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitin Codex mai aiki/na tsoho, bayanan tantancewa, saitunan Codex-lb, ko zaɓin mai samarwa.               |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin bayanan martaba na Claude Code na `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` ta atomatik daga kundin kai-tsaye. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitin Claude mai aiki/na tsoho, bayanan tantancewa, ko zaɓin mai samarwa. |

### Misalin Docker

```bash
# Ɗora binary na mahaɗin cikin kwantenar sannan ka gaya wa OmniRoute inda suke:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` yana fara aiki ne kawai idan a zahiri an yi bind-mount na hanyar daga
host — haɗa shi da mounts kamar `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (duba profile na
`host` a cikin `docker-compose.yml`). Hanyar da ba ta cikin home na mai amfani da
container kuma ba bind mount ba ce, ana yin watsi da ita, saboda duk abin da aka
rubuta a wurin zai ɓace idan aka sake ƙirƙirar container.

Image ɗin yana aiki a matsayin `USER node`, don haka `/root` da ba a mount ba **ba**
ingantaccen override ba ne.

| Variable                                 | Tsoho           | Source File                         | Bayani                                                                                                                                                       |
| ---------------------------------------- | --------------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(ta atomatik)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Tilasta gano container ya kasance a kunne (`1`/`true`) ko a kashe (`0`/`false`). Ana buƙatar wannan ne kawai a runtimes da ganowa ta atomatik ba ta gano ba. |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Ba da damar CLI-tool ya rubuta config zuwa hanyar container da ba a mount ba duk da haka. Makwafinsa na CLI shi ne `--allow-container-write`.                |

### Mataimakan CLI Binary (`omniroute`)

Waɗannan variables suna daidaita halayen CLI binary na `omniroute` kansa (ba ganowar
sidecar da ke sama ba).

| Variable                     | Tsoho             | Source File                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`             | _(na tsarin)_     | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Tilasta harshen fitarwar CLI. Locale na BCP-47 (misali `en`, `pt-BR`). Yana maye gurbin locale env vars na tsarin (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`         | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` domin tura stdout/stderr na server zuwa terminal a supervised mode. Daidai yake da flag ɗin `--log` a kan `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`        | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token na tabbatar da na'ura da ake saka a matsayin header na `x-omniroute-cli-token`. Ana ƙirƙirar sa ta atomatik a task 8.12.                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Iyakar lokacin HTTP na kowane yunƙuri (ms) don buƙatun CLI → server.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS` | `60000`           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Matsakaicin lokacin (ms) da CLI ke jiran health endpoint na server kafin ya buga gargaɗin timeout. Yana da amfani ga cold starts masu jinkiri (misali Windows). Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ta `--ready-timeout`.                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`          | `0`               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Saita zuwa `1` domin buga bayanan binciken retry/backoff zuwa stderr yayin umarnin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`      | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Directory na musamman don gano plugins na CLI (packages na `omniroute-cmd-*`). Yana komawa `~/.omniroute/plugins/` idan ba a saita shi ba. Na CLI kawai — ba ya taɓa isa ga plugin scanner na ɓangaren server, wanda `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` ke nuna masa hanya (sashe na 2). |

| Sauyi                                           | Tsoho                                               | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Yana soke ganowa ta atomatik ta localhost.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Manhajojin MCP/A2A                                          | Maɓallin API don kiran kayan aikin MCP na ciki da ƙwarewar A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID na maɓalli don danganta rajistar binciken MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Na gado                                                     | Tsohon madadin suna na `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Yawan kwanakin da ake riƙe tarihin ayyukan A2A a ma'ajiyar bayanai ta cikin gida kafin sharewar yau da kullum ta goge layi. Idan ba a saita ba, ba lamba ba ne, ko `<= 0`, zai koma `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Makunnin kashe fasalin lura da memory-hits na A2A. Saita zuwa `0` don tsallake binciken dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa gaba ɗaya ga wani aiki; kowace ƙima dabam (har da rashin saitawa) tana barin fasalin a kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Yana kunna ƙarshen recorded-triage na Issue Agent mai aiki ba tare da intanet ba/a cikin gida. Bar shi a kashe sai idan ana gudanar da tsarin aikin recorded-triage na cikin gida a bayyane.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Iyakar lokaci (ms) don gudu guda na recorded-triage na Issue Agent. Ana iyakance shi zuwa matsakaicin ƙimar ciki; yana komawa tsohuwar ƙimar da aka gina a ciki idan ba a saita shi ba ko ba ingantacce ba ne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(mahallin da ke aiki)_                             | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Mahalli/furofayil na yanayin nesa na CLI don umarnin `omniroute`; yana soke mahallin da ke aiki a ma'ajiyar mahallai ta cikin gida. Daidai yake da `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Kashe backend na OS-keychain na `keytar` na zaɓi don bayanan shaidar mahallin CLI. Idan an kunna, bayanan shaidar suna kasancewa cikin yanayin `config.json` `0600`, kuma CLI yana fitar da gargaɗin komawa madadi sau ɗaya; an tanade shi don aikin headless/container da aka yi da gangan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `true`                                              | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Tilasta ikon samun dama bisa scope a kan kiran kayan aikin MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

Ƙimomin kashewa: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazarar binciken iyakar ƙimar mai samarwa da kason amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazara (ms) tsakanin jere-jeren ɗauko kason OAuth a aikin aiki tare na rukuni; ana ɗauko haɗin OAuth ɗaya bayan ɗaya don kauce wa tururuwa zuwa sabis na sama. `0` yana hana wannan (a lokaci guda).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Mafi ƙarancin tazara (ms) tsakanin jere-jeren ɗauko kason sabis na sama a kan hanyar dubawa ta farko/sa ido ta kowace buƙata; yana rarraba kiran hanyar sadarwa da ke gudana lokaci guda don kada asusu da yawa daga IP guda su yi tururuwa zuwa sabis na sama. An haɗa shi cikin masu ɗauko kason Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (wuraren ɗauko bayanai duka biyu), OpenCode, da Crof (#6009, #6911). Har yanzu ba a rufe hanyar turawa ta gama-gari ta `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/da sauransu) ba — ana bibiyarta daban. Bai shafi samun bayanai daga ma'ajiyar wucin gadi ba. `0` yana kashewa; ana iyakance shi zuwa `0..5000`. |

Mafi ƙaranci `10000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_EXPORT_CRON`                     | `0 * * * *`                                         | `src/lib/jobs/logExportJob.ts`                              | Bayanin Cron (UTC) na aikin fitar da rajistan kira wanda ke kwashe bayanai zuwa kowane wurin fitar da rajista da aka kunna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_ENABLED`          | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Babban makunnin circuit-breaker na kowane engine a stacked-pipeline na T02. **Ana kunnawa bisa zaɓi (a kashe ta tsohuwa)** — idan yana kunne, za a tsallake engine da ke yawan jefa kuskure a buƙatu daban-daban (fail-open) na wani lokacin hucewa; a kashe = halayyar gargajiya mai bytes iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_THRESHOLD`        | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Yawan gazawar buƙatu daban-daban a jere kafin breaker na engine ya buɗe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_MS`      | `30000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Adadin milliseconds da za a ci gaba da tsallake engine mai buɗaɗɗen breaker kafin gwajin half-open.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_RETRIEVAL_RAMP_FACTOR`         | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Hawan martanin maidowa na T08/H8 CCR: kowace maidowar baya ta wani block da aka adana tana ƙara ingantaccen `minChars` nasa a layi madaidaici (abun ciki da ake yawan maidowa yana raguwa da matsawa; maidowa `>=3` = ba za a taɓa matsawa ba). `1` yana kashe hawan (tsallakewar binary kawai a matakin iyaka).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_DURABLE_STORE`                 | `true`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ma'ajiyar tubalan CCR mai ɗorewa (#9061). Tana tallafa wa ma'ajiyar cikin ƙwaƙwalwa da SQLite domin tubali ya tsira daga cirewar LRU, TTL, sake farawa, ko aikin dawo da bayanai da ya sauka a wani instance. Saita `false` don ajiye tubalan a cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai. Tubalan da suka wuce 512KB da cloud runtimes za su ci gaba da kasancewa cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai ba tare da la'akari da wannan ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_ENABLED`             | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Babban maɓallin daskare prefix na T08/H5 bisa lura da amfani. **Na zaɓi ne (a kashe yake ta tsohuwa)** — idan yana kunne, system prompt da aka lura da shi `>=` iyakar zai zama tsayayyen prefix da za a iya adanawa a cache, kuma za a kare shi daga matsawa har ma ga providers da static cache heuristic bai gano ba (daskarewa tana _karewa_ kawai, ba ta taɓa canza bayanai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_THRESHOLD`           | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Yawan lokutan lura da system prompt kafin a ɗauke shi a matsayin tsayayyen prefix da aka daskare.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BOOTSTRAPPED`                        | `false`                                             | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`                    | Bootstrap script yana saita `true` bayan saitin farko. Yana sarrafa bayyanar setup wizard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_BODY_PROJECT_OVERRIDE`         | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/antigravity.ts`                         | Hanyar kaucewa: ba da damar request body ya maye gurbin filin project na Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CREDITS`                           | `off`                                               | `open-sse/services/antigravityCredits.ts`                   | Manufar credits ta Google One AI: `off` ba ya taɓa saka credits, `retry` yana saka su sau ɗaya bayan kuskuren quota 429 da ya cancanta, sannan `always` yana saka su a request na farko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_ALLOW_SIGNATURE_BYPASS`            | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-gemini.ts`           | Ba wa fassarar request ta Antigravity damar tsallake tsauraran matakan tabbatar da request-signature na CLI idan upstream ya ƙi ainihin signatures (yanayin debug/tsohon-CLI). Ƙimar da ba sifili ba tana kunna wannan tsallakewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `AGY_TOKEN_FILE`                                | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token` | `src/app/api/providers/agy-auth/apply-local/route.ts`       | Maye gurbin hanyar token-file ta Antigravity CLI (agy) don shigo da local login da aka gano ta atomatik.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

### Gadar OAuth CLI (Ta Ciki)

| Variable            | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Asali                 | Bayani                                         |
| ------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | auto-detect       | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL na server don gadar auth ta CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Auth token don gadar CLI.                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | User ID don sessions na gadar CLI.             |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.            |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.             |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.           |

## 11. Bayanan Shaidar Mai Ba da OAuth

Bayanan shaida da aka tanada a ciki don **haɓakawa a localhost**. Don turawa zuwa sabar nesa, yi rajistar naka a na'urar sarrafa masu haɓakawa ta kowane mai ba da sabis.

| Sauyi                             | Mai ba da sabis                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | Claude Code (Anthropic)         | Abokin ciniki na jama'a — ba a buƙatar sirri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`        | Claude Code                     | Sauya URI na turawa. Tsoho: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`           | Codex / OpenAI                  | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | Gemini (Google)                 | Yana buƙatar `_SECRET` mai dacewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`      | Gemini (Google)                 | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`     | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)          | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`     | Antigravity (Google)            | Yana buƙatar `_SECRET` mai dacewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` | Antigravity (Google)            | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | GitHub Copilot                  | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`     | GHE Copilot                     | Zaɓin sauya ID na abokin cinikin OAuth na GitHub Enterprise Copilot. Idan ba a saita shi ba, yana komawa ga tsohon ƙimar jama'a ta `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`          | GitHub Copilot                  | Zaɓin sauya ID na haɗa abokin cinikin GitHub Copilot da ake aikawa a cikin kanun `Copilot-Integration-Id` da `Editor-Plugin-Version`. Tsohuwar ƙimarsa ita ce `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8)         | Madadin maɓallin API da `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` ke amfani da shi idan babu bayanan shaida na kowane haɗi. Na zaɓi ne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                   | Devin CLI (v3.8)                | Hanyar al'ada zuwa fayil ɗin binary na Devin CLI (`devin`). `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` ne ke gano ta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`      | GitLab Duo (v3.8)               | ID na abokin cinikin OAuth don GitLab Duo. Yi rajistar manhaja a `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` tare da URI na turawa `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` da izini `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Yana komawa ga `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`  | GitLab Duo (v3.8)               | Sirrin abokin cinikin OAuth don GitLab Duo. Na zaɓi ne — tsarin PKCE ba ya buƙatar sirri. Yana komawa ga `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`             | GitLab Duo (v3.8)               | Sauya tushen URL na GitLab (GitLab da aka karɓi baƙuncinsa da kai). Tsohuwar ƙimarsa ita ce `https://gitlab.com`. Yana komawa ga `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                 | GitLab Duo (v3.8)               | Tsohon madadi na `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. Ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita nau'in `_DUO_` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | GitLab Duo (v3.8)               | Madadin tsohon tsari na `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`      | GitLab Duo (v3.8)               | Madadin tsohon tsari na `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`       | Qoder                           | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`       | Qoder                           | Saita don kunna Qoder OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`           | Qoder                           | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`        | Qoder                           | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`           | Qoder                           | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`     | Qoder                           | Madadin maɓallin API kai tsaye (yana kauce wa OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`             | Qoder                           | ID na wurin aiki don Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`       | Qoder                           | Laƙabi na `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`            | Qoder                           | Sauya kundin daidaitawar Qoder CLI (zaman PAT mai zaman kansa, yana hana lalata shigar da aka yi ta burauza).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`    | Blackbox Web                    | Token na `tk` na frontend da za a aika a matsayin `validated` zuwa `/api/chat`. Ana buƙatarsa idan Blackbox yana tilasta daidaituwar token; in ba haka ba, OmniRoute zai koma amfani da UUID na bazuwar. Duba batu #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`          | Shingen kariya na Vision Bridge | URL na tushe mai dacewa da OpenAI don kiraye-kirayen vision-bridge da ba na Anthropic ba. Ta tsohuwa yana amfani da env na URL na OpenAI na tsohon tsari ko api.openai.com. Nuna shi zuwa madaukin kai na `/v1` na OmniRoute ko kowace mahadar da ta dace da OpenAI (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Batu #2232. Lokacin da URL ɗin ya kasance `/v1` na OmniRoute kansa, ƙaramin buƙatar bayyana yana aika `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` kuma yana tabbatar da izini da bayanan shaidar madaukin kai da aka warware (`sk_omniroute` sentinel a yanayin gida, ko `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) domin turawar `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` ta yi aiki. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`           | Shingen kariya na Vision Bridge | Maɓallin API don URL na sama. Yana rinjayar env vars na OpenAI / Google na kowane mai samarwa don kiraye-kirayen vision-bridge da ba na Anthropic ba. Samfuran Anthropic suna ci gaba da amfani da keɓaɓɓiyar hanyar maɓallin Anthropic. Batu #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Je zuwa [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Ƙirƙiri OAuth 2.0 Client ID (nau'i: "Manhajar yanar gizo")
> 3. Ƙara URL na sabarku a matsayin URI na turawa mai izini
> 4. Sauya ƙimomin bayanan shaida a cikin `.env`.

## 12. Sauya User-Agent na Masu Bayarwa

Sauya kanun `User-Agent` da ake aikawa zuwa kowane mai bayarwa na sama. Ajin tushe na executor ne ke ƙayyade wannan a lokacin aiki:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Tushen:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Sauyi | Ƙimar Tsoho | Lokacin Sabuntawa |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.258 (external, cli)` | Lokacin da Anthropic ta fitar da sabon sigar CLI |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Saita zuwa `1`/`true` don tura sunayen kayan aikin harness na ɓangare na uku kai tsaye zuwa Anthropic a duk hanyoyin da ke zuwa Anthropic (OAuth na asali da CLIProxyAPI). A tsoho, executor yana ba sunayen kayan aikin da ba na Claude Code ba laƙabi ta ƙayyadaddiyar hanya (yana amfani da daidaitaccen taswirar Claude Code idan akwai, in ba haka ba PascalCase), sannan ya mayar da sunayen zuwa na asali a cikin amsa ta hanyar `_toolNameMap`, don kada a ƙi harnesses masu kayan aikin snake_case a matsayin clients na ɓangare na uku da aka gane ta sawun yatsa. Don debugging kawai. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.142.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Lokacin da OpenAI ta sabunta Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.131.0` | Sauya sigar Codex client ba tare da dogaro da cikakken rubutun UA ba |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.258` | Sauya sigar Claude Code da ake sanarwa ba tare da dogaro da `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` ba. Anthropic tana taƙaita wasu models bisa wannan ƙimar (#12417). |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.81-6` | Sauya sigar Copilot CLI da ake sanarwa ba tare da dogaro da `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` ba |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Lokacin da GitHub Copilot Chat ya samu sabuntawa |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Lokacin da Antigravity IDE ya samu sabuntawa |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Lokacin da Kiro IDE ya samu sabuntawa |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Sauya `clientId` (ID na jama'a) na social device-code na Kiro |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Zaɓin shiga: cikakken tabbatar da CRC na kowane frame a kan Kiro event stream (don debugging na streams da suka lalace) |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Lokacin da Qoder CLI ya samu sabuntawa |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Lokacin da Cursor ya samu sabuntawa |

> [!TIP]
> Za ka iya ƙara sauye-sauyen User-Agent ga **kowane** mai bayarwa ta amfani da tsarin `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Executor yana ƙirƙirar sunan env var ta atomatik.
