## 13. Daidaituwar Sawun Yatsa na CLI

Idan aka kunna, OmniRoute yana sake tsara taken HTTP da filayen jikin JSON domin su dace da ainihin sa hannun kayan aikin CLI na hukuma. Wannan yana rage haɗarin yi wa asusu alama, tare da kiyaye IP na proxy ɗinka.

**Tushe:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Na Kowane Mai Bayarwa

| Sauyi                    | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                           |
| ------------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cline          |

### Na Gabaɗaya

| Sauyi            | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                                             |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`    | Kunna daidaituwar sawun yatsa ga **duk** masu bayarwa lokaci guda. |

### Sauya bayanan asalin Kimi Coding CLI

| Sauyi                   | Tsoho                   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`                | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya sigar Kimi CLI da ake aikawa yayin kiran OAuth/API.                             |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(tsohon da aka ɗauka)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya ID na na'urar Kimi da aka ɗauka, wanda ake amfani da shi a taken abokin ciniki. |

> [!NOTE]
> Wannan fasalin yana aiki tare da sauye-sauyen User-Agent (§12). Tsarin sawun yatsa yana kula da jerin taken da jerin filayen jiki, yayin da sauye-sauyen User-Agent ke kula da takamaiman zaren UA. Ana iya kunna kowannensu da kansa.

---

## 14. Masu Bayar da Maɓallin API

Maɓallan API na masu bayarwa waɗanda ke amfani da tantancewa kai tsaye. **Saitin da aka fi so:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Saita su ta hanyar sauye-sauyen muhalli wata hanya ce ta daban don girkawar Docker ko tsarin da ba shi da dubawar allo.

Tsarin da ake ganewa: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Sauyi              | Mai Bayarwa                                         |
| ------------------ | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                            |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                          |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + madadin Reader)           |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (laƙabi na `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)               |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + madadin taɗi |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (laƙabi na `GEMINI_API_KEY`)                 |

> [!NOTE]
> An cire shigarwar `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` na dindindin don Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, da Qianfan a v3.8.0 saboda runtime ba ya ƙara karanta su — waɗannan masu bayarwa suna dogara ne kawai da Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB mai ɓoyewa. Duba sashen _Bincike: Sauye-sauyen da Aka Cire / Marasa Amfani_ a ƙasan wannan takarda don hanyar ƙaura.

> [!TIP]
> Maɓallan da aka saita ta Dashboard ana adana su da ɓoyewa a SQLite kuma suna da fifiko kan sauye-sauyen muhalli.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings na `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, da `jina-search` ba sa cajin maɓallin muhallin cluster idan akwai haɗin dashboard na `jina-ai` (ko `jina-reader` na bai ɗaya) — `getProviderCredentials` yana cike na farko. Ana amfani da `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Katin Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) ba ya taɓa ba da `/v1/embeddings` ko `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (laƙabi `google/gemini-embedding-2`) yana fara amfani da haɗin `gemini` na dashboard. Ana amfani da `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Zirga-zirgar multimodal ta asali tana amfani da `x-goog-api-key` zuwa `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — abubuwan OpenAI `input` guda N suna zama vectors guda N.

---

## 15. Saitunan Ƙarewar Lokaci

Duk ƙimomi suna cikin **milliseƙan**. Ana warware su a wuri guda cikin `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Tsarin Matsayi na Ƙarewar Lokaci

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (sauyin duniya baki ɗaya)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (kiran masu samarwa na sama, tsoho: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 15000)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 1260000; 0 yana kashewa)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (yana iyakance tsawaitawar shiri mai daidaitawa, tsoho: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (an samo shi, tsoho: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 0 = a kashe)
```

| Maɓalli                                         | Tsoho                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Gajeriyar hanya ta gama-gari — tana maye gurbin tsoffin ƙimomin `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Jimillar wa'adin ƙarewar buƙatar HTTP don kiraye-kirayen zuwa mai samarwa na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin shiru tsakanin ɗanyen bytes daga sama kafin a katse. Samfuran tunani mai zurfi ba safai suke dakatawa fiye da sakan 90 ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Matsakaicin jimillar tsawon rayuwar rafin SSE mai aiki; ba ya sake farawa saboda bytes daga sama kuma ba ya dogara da `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. An samo shi daga mafi girman `timeoutMs` na kowane samfuri a rajista (1200000, Codex), tare da ƙarin tazara ta 60000, domin kada a kashe samfurin da aka ba shi damar amfani da cikakken kasafin lokacinsa a tsakiyar amsa. Saita zuwa `0` don kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(a kashe)_                                                       | Ko OmniRoute zai iya fitar da layukan sharhin SSE na `:` (misali bugun-zuciyar `: keepalive` da ƙarin bayanan metadata na `x-omniroute-*`). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa (#10524), saboda tsauraran clients masu dacewa da OpenAI suna yin JSON.parse ga kowane layin SSE kuma su lalace idan suka haɗu da sharhin `:`; wannan bai shafi bugun-zuciyar `data:` ba. Saita `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` don sake amincewa da shi. `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` yana amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Lokacin jiran karɓar aukuwar SSE ta farko wadda ba ping ba. Yana gaji `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Matsakaicin taga mai daidaitawa na jiran aukuwar farko don manyan buƙatun yawo, masu yawan kayan aiki, ko masu zurfin tunani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Maɓallin kashewa na heuristic ɗin `/goal`. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe ganowa gaba ɗaya — ba za a taɓa ƙara wa'adin shiryewa da dawo da rafi bisa jikin buƙata/headers ba, wanda ke rage haɗarin client ya ƙara wa'adin lokaci yadda yake so.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin taga na jiran aukuwar farko don ayyukan agent na `/goal` da aka gano ko buƙatun da aka tilasta da `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Kunna dawo da rafi da wuri ta atomatik don ayyukan agent na `/goal` da aka gano. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe amincewar musamman ta goal. Wannan zai iya ƘARA dawo da rafi ne kawai a kan tsohon saitin mai gudanarwa — ba zai taɓa soke fitowa daga tsarin da aka bayyana a sarari ta saitunan `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Cire aukuwar SSE na `codex.*` marasa daidaito (misali `codex.rate_limits`) waɗanda suke lalata `responses.stream()` na OpenAI SDK da kuskuren 502. Yana kunne ta tsohuwa (#11014). Saita `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` don tura su gaba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Zaɓin shiga sufuri na Codex app-server. Endpoint na WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) na sidecar na `codex app-server` na gida. Idan an saita shi tare da token, ana bi da buƙatun Codex ta JSON-RPC zuwa sidecar maimakon HTTP Responses API. Hakanan ana iya saitawa ga kowane haɗi ta `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` yana amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Inline capability/bearer token da ake gabatarwa ga app-server. Sauyawa ga kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Hanyar fayil mai ɗauke da capability token na app-server (daga `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). Ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN` ba. Sauyawa ga kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Kundin aiki da zagayen app-server ke gudana a cikinsa. Sauyawa ga kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufofin amincewa da ake miƙawa ga zagayen app-server (misali `never`, `on-request`). Sauyawa ga kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufofin sandbox da ake miƙawa ga zagayen app-server (misali `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Idan ba a saita ba, executor yana amfani da `workspace-write` a matsayin tsoho (an ƙarfafa tsaro; a baya `danger-full-access`). Sauyawa ga kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Amincewa ta atomatik da buƙatun amincewa na app-server da kansa (aiwatar da command/file/permission a kan host). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — ana ƙin buƙatun ta atomatik; wannan bai shafi kiran kayan aikin harness ba (suna bi ta wata hanyar wucewa ta `item/tool/call` ta daban). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`yes`. Sauyawa ga kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin jiran karɓar response headers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran fara amsa (ms) ga kowane yunƙurin kai-tsaye ba tare da proxy ba. Idan lokaci ya ƙare, za a sake gwadawa sau ɗaya ta sabon socket; saita `0` don kashe iyakar kuma a ci gaba da halin da ya gabata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (minti 10)                                               | Iyakar lokaci (ms) ga yunƙurin RETRY ta sabon socket bayan lokacin jiran fara amsa na yunƙurin da aka yi ta pooled socket a sama ya ƙare (#13703). Wannan yana aiki ne kawai idan mai kiran ya riga ya haɗa nasa siginar wa'adin ƙarewa (jerin fifikon haɗi/model/provider/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da aka warware); wannan siginar ita ce ainihin iyaka kuma ita ce ke fara aiki a hanyar da aka tsara, saboda haka wannan kariya ce mai yalwa maimakon tsayayyar iyaka — ba tare da ita ba, sake gwadawar tana amfani da gajeriyar taga iri ɗaya ta `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` kamar yunƙurin pooled socket, kuma tana mayar da 504 ga lafiyayyun models na reasoning masu jinkirin TTFB. Ba a taɓa yarda ta kasance ƙasa da tsayayyar mafi ƙarancin iyakar da ke sama ba; idan mai kiran bai bayar da wata siginar wa'adin ƙarewa ba kwata-kwata, sake gwadawar za ta bar tsayayyar mafi ƙarancin iyakar ba tare da canji ba. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar cikakken jikin amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin kafa haɗin TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Iyakar lokacin da keep-alive socket zai kasance ba ya aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Iyakar lokacin TLS fingerprint proxy (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Yana iyakance lokacin jiran byte na farko musamman a jikin bayanan hanyar safarar TLS-fingerprint ta wreq-js; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` kaɗai ba zai iya gano jikin bayanan da ya tsaya cik ba, domin yana kammala da zarar headers sun iso (#12656). Idan lokaci ya ƙare, za a soke wreq reader sannan a koma ga direct/proxy dispatcher; `0` yana kashe watchdog.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Na OpenCode executor kawai, kuma kawai yayin da feature flag na `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` yake kunne (a kashe yake ta tsohuwa): yana iyakance jiran byte na farko na jikin amsar Responses da ake stream bayan headers ɗinta (#13484). Stream na Responses yana buɗewa da `response.created`, don haka rashin wani bayani bayan wannan taga yana nuna tsayawa cik: za a sanyaya asusun sannan a juya buƙatar zuwa asusu na gaba sau ɗaya; tsayawa cik karo na biyu zai sa ta gaza nan take. `0` yana kashe kariyar ko da flag ɗin yana kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | Na OpenCode executor kawai: a ajiye buƙatar tare da heartbeat bayan maimaitattun kurakuran 429 na wucin gadi (ko sabon pool-strain marker), sannan a sake gudanar da sashe guda mai iyaka da ya ƙunshi har zuwa asusu 3 a jere maimakon watsa buƙatar ga dukkan asusun gaba ɗaya (#13924). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa: kowane 429 yana juya zuwa asusu na gaba daidai kamar da.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Na OpenCode executor kawai: sauya path na pool-strain marker da ake karantawa kafin a ajiye buƙatar (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, tsoho `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Sabon marker yana ajiye buƙatar ba tare da sake ƙirgawa ba; idan babu shi ko ya tsufa, za a koma ga burst counter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin proxy hop ga buƙatun bridge na `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                            | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Nuna Firecrawl web-fetch executor zuwa instance da aka host da kai (API key ba dole ba ne idan ba a cloud ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `FIRECRAWL_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `30000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin kowace buƙata ga Firecrawl web-fetch executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `300000`                                                          | Iyakar jimillar lokacin buƙatar server ga bridge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                                                           | Lokacin aika headers na amsa ta bridge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Iyakar lokacin da keep-alive na bridge zai kasance ba ya aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Iyakar lokacin raw socket (0 = a kashe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin rangwame bayan SIGTERM/SIGINT kafin a tilasta fita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | Ƙimar fallback da `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` ke amfani da ita idan ba a saita `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Iyakar lokaci (ms) ga presets na `validationRead` da `modelsProbe` a cikin `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Ƙara shi ga endpoints masu jinkiri (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) don hana sauyawa akai-akai tsakanin active/error a dashboard. Yana komawa zuwa 8000ms ga ƙimomin da ba su inganta ba (<1000) ko waɗanda ba lambobi ba ne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `25000`                                                           | Wa'adin ƙarewar lokacin fetch na musamman ga relay a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Dole ne relay da ya maƙale ya gaza kafin wa'adin abokin ciniki/agent (~30s), domin masu kira su ga gazawa ta musamman ga relay maimakon wa'adin gama-gari na upstream ya ƙare. An iyakance shi zuwa `29000` domin koyaushe ya fara aiki kafin sauran.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Jinkirin sake-gwadawa na bai ɗaya don hanyoyin sake-gwadawa sau ɗaya na direct/relay/proxy a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = sake gwadawa nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                                                           | Wa'adin ƙarewar buƙatar wreq-js ta asali (`claudeTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin rangwamen wa'adin ƙarshe na JS da aka ɗora a kan wa'adin asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Wa'adin ƙarewar buƙatar wreq-js ta asali (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin rangwamen wa'adin ƙarshe na JS da aka ɗora a kan wa'adin asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                    | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Yana ƙara "Kana da binciken yanar gizo da aka gina a ciki. Amsa tambayoyi kai tsaye ta amfani da sakamakon bincike." ga saƙon tsarin mai kira (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — Perplexity yana bincike ko ta yaya, kuma jimlar tana bayyana a cikin amsoshi a matsayin sharhin meta ga abokan cinikin coding. Saita `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` don maido da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `60000`                                                           | Wa'adin ƙarewar buƙatar wreq-js ta asali (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin rangwamen wa'adin ƙarshe na JS da aka ɗora a kan wa'adin asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Wa'adin ƙarewar buƙatar wreq-js ta asali (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` yana ɗaga shi ga kowace buƙata zuwa `180000` don dogayen samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin rangwamen wa'adin ƙarshe na JS da aka ɗora a kan wa'adin asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Rumbun burauzar Playwright na bai ɗaya don tattaunawar web-cookie mai amfani da burauza (`browserPool.ts`); saita `off` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Hanya zuwa binary na `obscura` da rumbun burauza da mai aiwatar da Cloudflare Playground ke amfani da shi a matsayin babban injin (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); ana gano shi ta atomatik daga PATH na tsarin idan ba a saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Nuna zuwa Obscura da ke riga yana gudana (`http://host:port`) maimakon ƙaddamar da wani; wannan module ba shi ne mai wannan process ɗin ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Tashar da aka fayyace don `obscura serve` da aka ƙaddamar; ana zaɓar tashar da ba a amfani da ita ta atomatik idan ba a saita ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Zaɓar da buƙatar tattaunawar web-cookie cikin hanyar da ke amfani da burauza (`browserBackedChat.ts`); `1` don kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | URL na tushe don mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (Connect-RPC na kimi.ai na ƙasa da ƙasa) (`kimi-web.ts`); sauya shi kawai don mirror/proxy endpoints.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Cikakken endpoint na tattaunawa don mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(ana gano shi ta atomatik)_                                      | Hanya zuwa executable na Chrome/Edge na tsarin don shiga ta burauza mai hulɗa na Adobe Firefly (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); yana maye gurbin ganowa ta atomatik ga kowane OS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _.build/ standalone output_                                       | Sauyin lokacin build don directory na standalone output da matakin haɗa-wuri bayan build ke amfani da shi (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); kayan aikin build ne, ba na runtime ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Ƙoƙarin combo target yana gado wa'adin ƙarewar buƙatar upstream da aka tantance (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ko
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan shi ne ke samar da tsohon ƙimar fetch). Saita `targetTimeoutMs` a cikin combo,
tsoffin ƙimomin combo, ko provider override kawai don sa combo fallback ya yi sauri; ƙimomin da suka fi
wa'adin upstream na yanzu ana iyakance su zuwa wa'adin upstream.

`comboTimeoutMs` wani wa'adin wall-clock ne na combo gaba ɗaya, daban, wanda ya shafi dukkan failover targets.
Bar shi ba a saita ba ko saita `0` don ci gaba da iteration marar iyaka (matakin dakatar da maƙalewa na minti 10 na
`COMBO_LOOP_SAFETY_TIMEOUT_MS` har yanzu yana aiki). Ƙima mai kyau tana maye gurbin wannan
kariyar ga combo. Ka sa `comboTimeoutMs` ya fi `targetTimeoutMs` tsawo domin failover
ya ci gaba da samun lokaci bayan target na farko mai jinkiri.

### Iyakokin Circuit Breaker

Daidaita circuit breaker a matakin provider. Tsoffin ƙimomin suna nuna ƙimomin da aka faɗaɗa da ake amfani da su tun daga v3.6 don haɗi 500+.

| Maɓalli                                                       | Tsoho     | Fayil ɗin Tushe                | Bayani                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu bayar da OAuth kafin mai katsewar ya kunna.                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Tagar sake saiti (ms) ga mai katsewar mai bayar da OAuth.                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu bayarwa masu amfani da API-key.                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Tagar sake saiti (ms) ga mai katsewar mai bayarwa mai amfani da API-key.                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu bayarwa na gida (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Tagar sake saiti (ms) ga mai katsewar mai bayarwa na gida.                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa na matakin mai bayarwa: yawan gazawa a cikin tagar kafin dukkan mai bayar da OAuth ya shiga lokacin jira.                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa na matakin mai bayarwa: tagar ƙirga gazawa mai motsi (ms) ga masu bayar da OAuth.                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa na matakin mai bayarwa: lokacin jira (ms) da zarar an kai iyakar mai bayar da OAuth.                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai bayar da OAuth yana shiga DEGRADED bayan gazawa masu wannan adadin.                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Matsakaicin ninkawar ƙara `resetTimeout` na mai bayar da OAuth.                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai bayar da OAuth yana ƙara mataki bayan zagayen buɗewa masu wannan adadin.                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa na matakin mai bayarwa: yawan gazawa a cikin tagar kafin dukkan mai bayarwa mai amfani da API-key ya shiga lokacin jira.                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa na matakin mai bayarwa: tagar ƙirga gazawa mai motsi (ms) ga masu bayarwa masu amfani da API-key.                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa na matakin mai bayarwa: lokacin jira (ms) da zarar an kai iyakar mai bayarwa mai amfani da API-key.                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai bayarwa mai amfani da API-key yana shiga DEGRADED bayan gazawa masu wannan adadin.                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Matsakaicin ninkawar ƙara `resetTimeout` na mai bayarwa mai amfani da API-key.                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai bayarwa mai amfani da API-key yana ƙara mataki bayan zagayen buɗewa masu wannan adadin.                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa na matakin mai bayarwa: yawan gazawa kafin dukkan mai bayarwa na gida ya shiga lokacin jira.                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa na matakin mai bayarwa: tagar ƙirga gazawa mai motsi (ms) ga masu bayarwa na gida.                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa na matakin mai bayarwa: lokacin jira (ms) da zarar an kai iyakar mai bayarwa na gida.                                                                    |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`   | Zurfin jinkirin maimaitawa da ake ɗaukar mai bayar da fil na ma'ajiyar mahallin a matsayin mara lafiya na dindindin, sannan a cire fil ɗin don sauyawa zuwa madadin. |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`   | Tagar hana saurin sauyawa (ms) wadda ke jure gajerun lokutan jira na wucin gadi kafin a cire fil na ma'ajiyar mahalli.                                               |

### Yanayi

| Yanayi                                         | Saitin                                                                  |
| ---------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ƙirƙirar lamba mai ɗaukar lokaci mai tsawo** | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (minti 15)                                  |
| **Ƙayyade jimillar tsawon rayuwar rafi**       | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (minti 21)                           |
| **Gaggauta gazawa ga API na samarwa**          | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                                     |
