## 16. Rubuta rajista

Tsarin rubuta rajista yana rubutawa zuwa stdout da kuma fayilolin rajista da ake juyawa. Ana karanta dukkan saituna daga `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Sauyi                                     | Tsoho                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Mafi ƙarancin matakin rajista: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Tsarin fitarwa: `text` (mai sauƙin karantawa ga mutum) ko `json` (mai tsari).                                                                                                                                             |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Rubuta rajistoci zuwa fayil tare da stdout.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Hanyar fayil ɗin rajista (dangane da tushen aikin ko `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                         |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Matsakaicin girman fayil kafin a juya shi. Yana karɓar: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ko bayit kai tsaye.                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Adadin kwanakin da za a riƙe fayilolin rajistar manhaja da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Matsakaicin adadin kwafin ajiyar fayilolin rajista da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Adadin kwanakin da za a riƙe bayanan rajistar buƙata/kira a ma'ajiyar bayanai.                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Matsakaicin adadin bayanan rajistar kira a ma'ajiyar wucin-gadi ta ƙwaƙwalwa.                                                                                                                                             |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin adadin layuka a teburin SQLite na `call_logs` kafin a rage su.                                                                                                                                                |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_          | Tilasta kunna ko kashe cikakken rajistar buƙatu, tare da ƙetare saitin dashboard.                                                                                                                                         |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (awa 1)          | Matsakaicin shekarun bayanan rajistar buƙatu masu aiki da suka zama marayu kafin tsaftacewar ƙwaƙwalwa.                                                                                                                   |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Adana gutsattsarin rafi a cikin kayan aikin pipeline lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Sai an zaɓa (`true`) — a kashe yake ta tsohuwa don adana sararin diski.                                                 |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Matsakaicin girman kayan aikin rajistar kiran pipeline a KB lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin adadin layuka a teburin SQLite na `proxy_logs` kafin a rage su.                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Haɗa IP na abokin ciniki/fitarwa da farkon sunayen asusu a rajistocin console na `[ProxyEgress]`. Bayanan rajistar proxy na dashboard/ma'ajiyar bayanai suna riƙe cikakkun bayanai.                                       |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (minti 1)          | Sau nawa `src/lib/logRotation.ts` ke sake duba girman fayil ɗin rajista mai aiki.                                                                                                                                         |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Matsakaicin tsawon kirtani da ake riƙewa a kayan aikin rajistar taɗi (tsoho 64 KB).                                                                                                                                       |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Adadin abubuwan jerin da ake riƙewa daga ƙarshensa lokacin taƙaita payload na rajistar taɗi.                                                                                                                              |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Matsakaicin zurfin cuɗanya kafin a taƙaita payload na rajistar taɗi.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Matsakaicin adadin maɓallan object da ake riƙewa a payload na rajistar taɗi (0 = babu iyaka).                                                                                                                             |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Girman dukan jikin buƙata/amsa (KB) kafin a maye gurbinsa da taƙaitaccen bayani kawai maimakon cikakken kwafi. Ƙara wannan idan dogayen tattaunawar wakili suna nuna alamar madadi maimakon ainihin saƙonnin a dashboard. |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Idan true ne, `serializeArtifactForStorage` yana tsallake taƙaitawa bisa girma. Don gyaran kurakurai kawai.                                                                                                               |

## 17. Inganta Amfani da Ƙwaƙwalwa

| Sauyawa                       | Tsoho                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`         | _ta atomatik_ (na’ura kai tsaye); **`1024` a cikin hoton Docker** | **Ƙimar da ake ba da shawara** ta iyakar heap na V8 (MB) don Docker/mai zaman kansa. Idan ba a saita ba, ana daidaita ta a lokacin aiki (~35% na RAM ɗin tsarin, tare da taƙaita ta zuwa `[512, 4096]`); `512` ita ce ƙaramar iyaka kawai idan ba za a iya karanta jimillar ƙwaƙwalwar ba. A kan `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), ana ƙara ƙima da aka saita **a sarari** a matsayin `--max-old-space-size`, kuma ita ce ke **rinjayar** tutar heap ta NODE_OPTIONS idan suna saɓani (tutar ƙarshe ta V8). Har yanzu `omniroute serve` yana fifita tutar heap da ke cikin NODE_OPTIONS (#5238). Kada a saita su biyun da lambobi daban-daban — tsarin yana rubuta gargaɗi da ke ambaton ƙimomin biyu da kuma wadda ta yi nasara. **Hoton Docker na hukuma koyaushe yana saita `1024`, don haka ba a taɓa gudanar da daidaitawar atomatik a cikinsa.** Coding-agent `/v1/responses` yana buƙatar `8192`–`12288` tare da sararin cgroup na kariya — duba [Jagorar Docker — RAM na lokacin aiki](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`       | `50`                                                              | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar system prompt da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`      | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                  | Matsakaicin jimillar girman cache na prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`         | `300000` (minti 5)                                                | TTL na shigarwar cache na prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`     | `100`                                                             | Matsakaicin adadin amsoshin temperature=0 da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`    | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                  | Matsakaicin jimillar girman semantic cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`       | `1800000` (minti 30)                                              | TTL na shigarwar semantic cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE` | `5`                                                               | Tushen local-corpus da ke riƙe da fihirisar cikin ƙwaƙwalwa mai aiki a lokaci guda (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: idan an kai iyaka, ana cire fihirisar tushen da aka fi daɗewa ba a yi amfani da shi ba, sannan a sake gina ta a tambaya ta gaba. Ana ɗora ƙaramar iyaka zuwa `1`; ƙimar da ba lamba ba tana komawa ga tsohuwar ƙima.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`          | `50`                                                              | Matsakaicin adadin abubuwan stream na baya-bayan nan a cikin buffer na kallon kai tsaye na Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`      | `128000`                                                          | Matsakaicin tsawon context na madadin gama-gari ga models da ba su da saitin kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`          | `100`                                                             | Ƙarin sararin token na kariya da ake tanada yayin bin diddigin ƙayyadaddun amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Matsewa

| Sauyawa                               | Tsoho         | Bayani                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | ba a saita ba | Amince da `.rtk/filters.json` na aikin ba tare da hash na `.rtk/trust.json` ba. Yi amfani da wannan kawai a ci gaban gida mai sarrafawa. |

### Injin Ƙwaƙwalwa (tsari 21)

### Kuɗin event loop na ƙwaƙwalwa, ƙwarewa, da sabunta token (#10349)

OmniRoute **tsarin Node guda ɗaya ne**. Ciro/dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa, shigar da ƙwarewa, da sabunta token na provider suna gudana a kan **event loop ɗin nan ɗaya** da `GET /healthz` da dashboard. Ba worker thread ba ne.

| Aiki                     | Lamba                              | Tsoho                                               | Ikon mai gudanarwa                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------ | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Ciro / dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa | `src/lib/memory/`                  | **memoryEnabled** na dashboard (a kunne ta tsohuwa) | Kashe **Settings → Memory**. Babu wani keɓaɓɓen maɓallin kashewa na env, sai dai kashe fasalin a cikin saituna.                                      |
| Shigar da skills         | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | **skillsEnabled** na dashboard (a kunne ta tsohuwa) | Kashe **Settings → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Saitunan sandbox da ke ƙasa suna iyakance aiwatarwa ne kawai bayan an riga an kunna shigarwar. |
| Sabunta token            | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | A kunne ga masu samar da OAuth/web da aka haɗa      | Cire haɗin mai samarwa ko a bar tokens su ci gaba da kasancewa masu inganci; a halin yanzu babu env mai suna `TOKEN_REFRESH=0`.                      |

Idan `/healthz` yana jinkiri a kan na'ura mara nauyi, da farko kashe memory + skills, sannan a duba nauyin catalog/compression (#10303, #9685). Waɗannan fasalolin suna ba da dama a wuraren `await`, amma har yanzu suna gasa don thread guda ɗaya.

Saitunan embedding layer, vector store da reranking na tsarin ƙwaƙwalwa mai ɗorewa (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Variable                             | Tsoho                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (minti 5)          | TTL na cache ɗin embedding na cikin ƙwaƙwalwar na'ura (ga kowace source/model/dim signature).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Matsakaicin shigarwar LRU da ake adanawa a cikin cache ɗin embedding.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | HF repo id na pipeline ɗin MiniLM na cikin gida na `@huggingface/transformers` da ake zaɓar kunnawa (~23 MB int8, ~400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | HF repo id na static potion/Model2Vec lookup-table embedder. Ana sauke shi ne lokacin da ake buƙata zuwa cache dir.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Kundin adireshin da ake amfani da shi don cache na fayilolin static potion model. Idan ba a saita ba, tsohuwar hanyarsa tana ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Sauya Hugging Face Hub base URL da `staticPotion.ts` ke amfani da shi (misali, mirror endpoint don saitunan da aka ware daga intanet).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Tsohon top-K da binciken vector na brute-force na `sqlite-vec` ke amfani da shi a cikin `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Reciprocal Rank Fusion constant `k` don haɗaɗɗen dawo da FTS5 + vector (tsarin sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Wurin gwaji/bincike a cikin `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): idan `true`, yana tilasta vector store ya koma `null` (yana kwaikwayon muhallin cloud/WASM ba tare da `sqlite-vec` ba), wanda ke rage dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa zuwa binciken keyword na FTS5. A bar shi ba tare da saiti ba a production.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_           | API key na backend na Notion (wanda sanannen backend preset na `genericBackend.ts` ke amfani da shi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Base URL na Notion API (ana iya sauya shi don madadin Notion da aka karɓi baƙuncinsa da kai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_           | API key na backend na Obsidian Vault (wanda sanannen backend preset na `genericBackend.ts` ke amfani da shi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | Base URL na Obsidian Vault API (ana iya sauya shi don vault na nesa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | Babban maɓallin TV6 typed memory decay. **Sai an zaɓi kunna shi (a kashe ta tsohuwa)** — sweep ɗin yana **goge** ƙwaƙwalwar da ta lalace. Idan a kashe yake, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` telemetry ne kawai kuma ba a taɓa goge komai ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (kwanaki) bayan wanda ƙwaƙwalwar `episodic` da ba a yi amfani da ita ba take lalacewa. `0` yana sa episodic ma ta zama kariyayyiya. Nau'ikan masu ɗorewa (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) koyaushe suna da kariya. Agogon lalacewar yana sake kafa tushe a kan `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Ƙwaƙwalwar da aka shigar sau `>=` wannan adadin tana samun kariya daga lalacewa ba tare da la'akari da nau'inta ba. `0` yana kashe kariyar samun dama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (a kashe)               | Tazara (daƙiƙoƙi) don sweep na lalacewa na lokaci-lokaci da za a iya zaɓar kunnawa a cikin `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/ba a saita ba = babu sweep na lokaci-lokaci. Sai an zaɓi kunnawa sau biyu: yana kuma buƙatar `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(ba a saita ba)_           | Provider ids da aka raba da waƙafi (ba tare da bambance manyan da ƙananan haruffa ba) waɗanda ke karɓar saƙon `system` **a index 0 kawai** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Ga waɗannan, cache-safe mid-array memory splice ba shi da aminci a tattaunawa mai zagaye da yawa, don haka ana haɗa ƙwaƙwalwar/sa ta a gaba a matsayin saƙon system na farko. Ta tsohuwa `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo` kawai; a faɗaɗa shi don endpoints masu jituwa da OpenAI da aka karɓi baƙuncinsu da kai (misali Qwen3.5+/3.6) waɗanda chat template ɗinsu ke tilasta wannan ƙa'idar saƙon system guda ɗaya a gaba. |

### Misalin Docker Mai Ƙarancin RAM

`128` na dashboard kawai ne. Coding agents a kan wannan heap suna samun `FATAL ERROR` yayin dogon `/v1/responses`. Kada a yi amfani da wannan misalin a matsayin gateway na Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

## Daidaita Arena ELO

| Sauyi                        | Tsoho         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_ENABLED`     | `true`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                     | Daidaita ELO na jadawalin jagororin Arena AI lokaci-lokaci, wanda za a iya saita shi daga Tutocin Fasali na Dashboard ko amfani da `false` don ƙin shiga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Tsarin prefix da ake amfani da shi ga ids na samfura a cikin `GET /v1/models`. `dual` yana nuna duka gajeren alias prefix da canonical provider prefix ga kowane samfuri (dacewa da sigogin baya — yana kusan ninka girman kasidar); `alias` yana fitar da id ɗaya ga kowane samfuri; `canonical` yana fitar da cikakken provider-id prefix kawai (masu samarwa waɗanda alias ɗinsu ya riga ya zama canonical id suna riƙe shigarwa ɗaya tak). Abokan hulɗa za su iya sauya wannan ga kowace buƙata ta amfani da `?prefix=alias`. Duba [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_INTERVAL`    | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/arenaEloSync.ts`                                                            | Tsakanin lokacin daidaitawa a cikin daƙiƙu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

## Mai Samar da Playground na PromptQL (Ba na Hukuma ba/Na Gwaji)

Gadar zaman GraphQL da aka sake gina ta hanyar nazarin tsarin prompt.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Dukansu na zaɓi ne — saitunan tsoho suna nuni zuwa endpoints na playground na jama'a; a sauya su kawai don tura PromptQL mai zaman kansa/wani dabam.

| Sauyi                        | Tsoho                                                              | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                         | Bayani                                                                        |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | GraphQL endpoint da ake amfani da shi don ayyukan taɗi/zama.                  |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | GraphQL endpoint da ake amfani da shi don neman ma'aunin kuɗin amfani/amfani. |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint da ake amfani da shi don sabunta token gwargwadon iyawa.             |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Matsakaicin lokaci (ms) na binciken `thread_events` kafin lokaci ya ƙare.     |

## Mai Bayar da HyperAgent na Yanar Gizo (Ba na Hukuma ba/Gwaji)

Gadar zama da aka gano ta hanyar nazarin baya don hyperagent.com (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Na zaɓi ne — ƙimar tsohuwa tana nuni zuwa maƙurar biyan kuɗi/amfani ta jama'a; a sauya ta kawai don turawar HyperAgent da aka ɗauki nauyinta da kai ko wata madadiya.

| ---------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `HYPERAGENT_USAGE_URL` | `https://hyperagent.com/api/settings/billing/usage` | `open-sse/services/usage/hyperagent.ts` | Maƙurar da ake amfani da ita don ɗauko tubalan ƙimar biyan kuɗi/amfani. |

## Ƙayyadaddun Amfani na Kilo Code

Duba ma'aunin USD na mutum da amfanin Kilo Pass don mai bayar da Kilo Code. Na zaɓi ne — ƙimar tsohuwa tana nuni zuwa Kilo API na jama'a; a sauya ta kawai don hanyar isarwa ko tsarin gwaji. Tantancewa tana amfani da alamar shiga ta OAuth da haɗin yake da ita.

| Sauyi          | Ƙimar Tsohuwa         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                       | Bayani                                                                                      |
| -------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KILO_API_URL` | `https://api.kilo.ai` | `open-sse/services/usage/kilocode.ts` | Tushen URL da ake amfani da shi don ɗauko ma'aunin Kilo Code na mutum da amfanin Kilo Pass. |

## Mai Bayar da Adobe Firefly na Yanar Gizo (Ba na Hukuma ba/Gwaji)

Sabunta zama ta hanyar burauza don mai bayar da Adobe Firefly na yanar gizo
(`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`,
`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Na zaɓi ne — an daidaita duk ƙimomin tsohuwa don shigarwar
kwamfutar tebur ta yau da kullum.

> **An cire a #9255.** Tsohon tsarin tafiyar Chrome mai haɗin CDP (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts) an
> maye gurbinsa da sabis ɗin shiga ta burauza na Playwright, kuma saitunansa ba sa samuwa. Sauye-sauyen
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART, tare da
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS da ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, ba a karanta su a ko'ina cikin
> tushen lambar — saita su ba ya yin wani tasiri.

| Sauyi                                | Ƙimar Tsohuwa     | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------ | ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Saita zuwa `1` don Chrome marar fuskar gani na gaske (an san cewa ba ya aiki don ƙirƙirawa; don gano matsala kawai).                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Shiga/fita daga dumama burauza tun da wuri. `0` yana kashe dumama tun da wuri (farfaɗowa daga 408 a tsakiyar rukuni har yanzu yana aiki). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Saita zuwa `0` don kashe adana zaman Adobe Firefly a faifai.                                                                              |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Mafi ƙarancin tazara (ms) da ake tilastawa tsakanin aika buƙatu a jere, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ƙarin tazara (ms) da ake ƙara bayan nasarar rukuni, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                                    |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Jinkiri na tushe (ms) kafin aika buƙatar ƙirƙirawa, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                                    |

## 19. Daidaita Model (Dev)

| Maɓalli                             | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tilasta kunna ko kashe daidaita farashin models.dev. Idan ba a saita ba = a bi Saituna > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no` **yana rinjayar DB** kuma yana tsallake daidaitawa ta lokaci-lokaci da binciken SQL/JSON na `getModelsDevPricing()` (don farfaɗowa idan dashboard ya maƙale a kan event loop ɗaya). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` yana tilasta kunna daidaitawa. Ajiye/share farashi har yanzu suna kiran `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, wanda ba ya yin komai ƙarƙashin takurawar mintuna 60 ko `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tazarar daidaita kundin model a lokacin haɓakawa, cikin daƙiƙu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Tazara (daƙiƙu) ga mai daidaita context-window mai gyara kansa (5004): yana liƙa windows da provider ya ayyana daga gano `/models` a matsayin sauye-sauyen `auto:discovery` idan sun bambanta da kundin. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa. Yana sake amfani da bayanan da aka riga aka daidaita (ba sabon fetch); ba ya taɓa maye gurbin sauye-sauyen `manual`.                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Kundin bayanansa yana ɗauke da bayanan shaidar OAuth/API na mai samarwa, kuma image ɗin da aka kafe ba shi da damar sauya `api-keys` na data-plane ta hanyar env (sai dai `config.yaml` da aka mount), don haka `0.0.0.0` yana fallasa sabis mai ɗauke da bayanan shaida ga dukkan LAN. |

| `DARIO_PORT`                                   | `3456`                                 | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Port na ginannen sabis na Dario.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| `DARIO_PORT`                                   | `3456`                                 | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Port na ginannen sabis na Dario.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `LOCAL_HOSTNAMES`                              | _(babu komai)_                         | `open-sse/config/providerRegistry.ts`                                              | Ƙarin sunayen masaukin da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda ake ɗauka a matsayin na “gida” (sunayen sabis na Docker, da sauransu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` na relays na ɓangare na uku ne kawai waɗanda ke karɓar clients na Claude Code
kaɗai. OmniRoute yana sake rubuta requests domin waɗannan relays su karɓe su. Idan kana son amfani da
Claude Code CLI kawai, ko kuma ba ka tabbatar da mene ne waɗannan relays ba, ka bar wannan a kashe sannan ka ƙara provider na yau da kullum
mai dacewa da Anthropic a maimakon haka.

---

Saita zuwa `0` don kashe tazarar; an iyakance shi zuwa 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Madadin `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` ne wanda ba ya lalata bayanai: nan take proxy ɗin zai fita daga warwarewar pool/rotation (tacewar alive-status da warwarewar scope-pool ke amfani da ita ta riga ta cire shi), sannan za a sake kunna shi ta atomatik da zarar ya fara cin gwajin probes kuma. Yana amfani da iyakar `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER` tare. Ana soke timer ɗin da zarar job ya fara gudana — ana iyakance gudanawar dabam ta `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`. | `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (an kashe)              | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                         | Iyakar karɓar request cikin layi: a ƙi da 429 `queue_full` da zarar adadin requests ɗin da ke layi ya kai wannan iyaka. `0` = babu iyaka (tsohuwar ƙima).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on` don kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Ana ƙara shi ta exponential gwargwadon gazawa masu jere. Ana amfani da shi ne kawai idan `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (minti 5)          | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                  | Matsakaicin iyakar lokacin dakatawa (ms) kafin a sake gwada mai samarwa/haɗin da ya gaza ba tare da la'akari da komai ba. Ana amfani da shi ne kawai idan `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (ƙimar farko) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (dabaru) | **Menene:** dawo da rafukan sama da suka yanke cikin bayyane ba tare da mai amfani ya lura ba (tashar free-claude-code). Yana riƙe taga SSE ta buɗewa har zuwa `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms), domin yankeewar _kafin-aikawa_ — wadda ke faruwa kafin kowane byte ya isa ga abokin ciniki — a sake buɗewa kuma a sake gwadawa a ɓoye. **Lokacin kunnawa:** idan rafukan sama ba su da tabbas/suna yawan yankewa da byte 0 a farkon rafi; a bar shi a KASHE idan ba za ku iya jure ƙarin lokaci har zuwa 750 ms kafin samun token na farko a kowane rafi ba. Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`. Yana saita ƙimar farko ta saitin Resilience da ake adanawa; saitin Dashboard ne ke da rinjaye da zarar an saita shi. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (ƙimar farko) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (dabaru) | **Menene:** ci gaba daga tsakiyar rafi (Mataki na 4.4) — bayan yankeewar _bayan-aikawa_ (bytes sun riga sun isa ga abokin ciniki), a sake yin buƙata tare da ɓangaren rubutun a matsayin cikewar farko ta mataimaki sannan a haɗa ƙarshen da ya ɓace. Don rafukan rubutu tsantsa masu dacewa da OpenAI kawai; ba ya taɓa aiki yayin da kiran kayan aiki ke gudana. **Lokacin kunnawa:** dogayen samarwar da ake yankewa a tsakiyar amsa, kuma kun yarda ƙarshen da aka dawo da shi ya iso gaba ɗaya lokaci guda maimakon token-bi-token. Ba ya dogara da `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (yanayin haɗari daban). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`.                                                                               |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`                    | Mai sa ido na zaɓi kan fitarwa mai amfani ta rafi mai aiki. Yana gano rafukan da ke ci gaba da aika gutsattsari amma suke ƙasa da ƙayyadadden saurin fitarwar mataimaki; siginonin ci gaba da aiki, abubuwan amfani, canje-canje marasa komai, da matakan kayan aiki/tunani ba sa nuna ci gaba na ƙarya. Ya bambanta da iyakokin lokacin rashin aiki da na ƙarshe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WARMUP_MS`            | `30000`                     | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Lokacin rangwame kafin tantance saurin fitarwa, wanda aka iyakance zuwa 0–600000 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                     | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Taga mai motsi ta fitarwa mai amfani, wadda aka iyakance zuwa 1000–600000 ms; ana buƙatar cikakkiyar taga guda kafin a dakatar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                         | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Mafi ƙarancin saurin byte na UTF-8 na fitarwar mataimaki (wakilin token mai taka-tsantsan), wanda aka iyakance zuwa 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                         | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Mafi ƙarancin samfurin fitarwa mai amfani da ba sifili ba wanda ake ɗauka a matsayin abin aunawa, wanda aka iyakance zuwa bytes 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                       | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                   | Mafi ƙarancin jinkirin bazuwar (ms) da ake ƙarawa kan `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` tsakanin binciken lafiyar token na masu samarwa, domin hana taruwar buƙatu lokaci guda (Matsala #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                      | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                   | Matsakaicin jinkirin bazuwar (ms) da ake ƙarawa kan `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` tsakanin binciken lafiyar token na masu samarwa, domin hana taruwar buƙatu lokaci guda (Matsala #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                        | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                        | Matsakaicin tazarar jira mai ƙaruwa (dakiku) tsakanin sake-gwadawar lokacin-jira.
