Zagayowar rayuwar dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) tana ƙaddamar da CLI na `headroom-ai` na cikin gida a kan loopback ta tsohuwa; a sauya wannan kawai don nuna wani proxy na Docker sidecar na waje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

### Kafaffen ƙimomin daidaita dawo da stream

Ana daidaita halayyar riƙewa ta dawo da stream ta hanyar kafaffen ƙimomi da aka rubuta kai tsaye a cikin
`open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), waɗanda aka nuna a nan domin tunani —
sauya su yana buƙatar gyaran code, ba env var ba:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — tsawon lokacin da ake riƙe taga ta buɗewar SSE
  domin a iya sake gwadawa idan an yanke ta da wuri kafin a miƙa ko da byte ɗaya ga abokin ciniki.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — iyaka mai tsauri ga taga da ake riƙewa; a miƙa
  (flush + passthrough) da zarar wannan adadin bytes ya taru, ba tare da la’akari da mai ƙidayar lokaci ba.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — matsakaicin adadin sake buɗe stream na upstream
  ba tare da bayyana wa mai amfani ba, muddin har yanzu ba a miƙa abin da aka riƙe ba.

> **Iyakance ƙimar sliding-window na kowane provider (babu env var):** _fallback_ na
> iyakance ƙimar sliding-window na kowane provider da aka ɗauko daga FCC yana cikin code
> (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, an haɗa shi ta hanyar
> `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) amma ana fitar da shi da **taswirar tsoho mara komai**
> kuma **ba shi da env var na operator** a halin yanzu — ana kunna shi ne kawai ta hanyar test hook /
> gyaran code. Da gangan ba a jera shi a teburin da ke sama ba. Mai iyakance relay na kowane `(token, IP)`
> wanda _yake_ da abin da za a daidaita shi ne `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

---

## 22. Gyaran Kurakurai

> [!CAUTION]
> Waɗannan variables suna samar da **cikakken output** kuma suna iya fallasa bayanai masu muhimmanci. **Kada a taɓa kunna su a production.**

| Variable                              | Tsoho                        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Saita `1` don kunna cikakkun logs na Cursor executor (SSE chunks da aka decode, da sauransu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Alias mai dacewa da tsofaffin nau'ikan `CURSOR_DEBUG`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Path na fayil na zaɓi wanda ke karɓar ɗanyen Cursor chunks da aka decode yayin da `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Lokacin jira kafin stream marar aiki ya ƙare (ms) ga Cursor executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                       | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ƙarin lokacin jira (ms) bayan composer kv_after_text soft terminator yayin da bytes suka rage a buffer — yana ba trailing exec_mcp tool call lokaci don kammala frame ɗinsa.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | a kunne (`!== "0"`)          | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Tool-commit directive da ke sa composer-2.5 ya yi tool calls cikin aminci. Saita `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION_APPEND` | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/translator/request/claude-to-openai.ts`, `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts` | Rubutun system prompt da operator ya ayyana wanda ake liƙawa ga system message BAYAN translation (post-translation injection), yana isa ga hanyoyin codex/Responses da `/v1/messages`. Haka kuma ana amfani da shi a matsayin directive prefix da ake cirewa daga echoed system preamble blocks. Bar shi ba a saita ba don kashewa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRIP_SYSTEM_PREAMBLE`     | `0` (a kashe)                | `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts`, `open-sse/utils/directivePreambleStripper.ts`     | Saita zuwa `1` don cire echoed system-prompt preamble blocks daga farkon openai→claude stream. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — heuristics ɗin sun dace da tsarin rubutun turanci kuma suna canza response payload, saboda haka reply da ya fara da irin wannan sashe bisa halacci zai rasa shi.                                           |
| `CURSOR_IMAGE_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `15000`                      | `open-sse/utils/cursorImages.ts`                                                                      | Lokacin jira kafin fetch ɗin kowane hoto ya ƙare (ms) ga remote `image_url` vision input.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CURSOR_STATE_DB_PATH`                | _(ana bincikawa)_            | `open-sse/utils/cursorVersionDetector.ts`                                                             | Sauya hanyar binciken state DB na Cursor IDE da ake amfani da ita don gano nau'in IDE.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_CLI_VERSION`            | _(gano / kulle)_             | `open-sse/utils/cursorAgentCliVersion.ts`                                                             | Agent CLI build id (`YYYY.MM.DD-<hash>`) don `x-cursor-client-version: cli-…` a Agent Run.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_BIN`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Path zuwa Cursor Agent binary da ake amfani da shi wajen samar da hoto. Idan ba a saita ba, handler yana amfani da `providerSpecificData.agentBin`, sannan PATH.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CURSOR_IMG_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `210000`                     | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Jimillar lokacin kowane hoto (ms) ga ayyukan hoton Cursor Agent.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CURSOR_IMG_MAX_CONCURRENT`           | `2`                          | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Ƙofar sarrafa ayyukan hoton Cursor da ke gudana lokaci guda a shared-seat.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CURSOR_IMG_MODEL`                    | buƙata / `auto`              | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Sauya Cursor CLI `--model` ga ayyukan hoto.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `UC_IMAGE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`           | `2000`                       | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/ucImage.ts`                                              | Tsakanin lokutan duba sakamakon samar da hoto na UC (uncensored.com) (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `UC_IMAGE_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `60000`                      | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/ucImage.ts`                                              | Jimillar lokacin duba sakamakon samar da hoto na UC kafin ya ƙare (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `UC_VIDEO_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`           | `3000`                       | `open-sse/handlers/videoGeneration/providers/ucVideo.ts`                                              | Tsakanin lokutan duba sakamakon samar da bidiyo na UC (uncensored.com) (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `UC_VIDEO_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                     | `open-sse/handlers/videoGeneration/providers/ucVideo.ts`                                              | Jimillar lokacin duba sakamakon samar da bidiyo na UC kafin ya ƙare (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CURSOR_DATA_DIR`                     | _(ana bincikawa)_            | `open-sse/utils/cursorAgentCliVersion.ts`                                                             | Sauya data dir na Cursor Agent CLI (`…/versions/<id>`); variable ɗaya ne da official agent ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_TOKEN`                        | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `scripts/ad-hoc/cursor-tap.cjs`                                                                       | Kai-tsaye Cursor bearer token da kayan aikin developer ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_REQUEST_SHAPE`         | a kashe (ana shiga ta `"1"`) | `src/app/api/v1/chat/completions/route.ts`                                                            | Yi log na content-type/length markers ga manyan chat payloads idan an saita `"1"`. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa don rage cunkoson log.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DEBUG_RESPONSES_SSE_TO_JSON`         | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/handlers/responseTranslator.ts`                                                             | Saita `true` don yin log na bayanan translation na Responses API SSE→JSON.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `DEBUG_CLAUDE_NONSTREAM`              | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/handlers/responseTranslator.ts`                                                             | Saita `true` don bayyana textContent chunks marasa komai a hanyar translation ta Claude response (don debugging kawai).                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_E2E_MODE`      | _(ba a saita ba)_            | Tsarin gwajin E2E                                                                                     | Saita `true` don kunna yanayin gwajin E2E (sassaucin auth, test hooks).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

## 23. Haɗin GitHub

Ba masu amfani damar kai rahoton matsaloli kai tsaye daga Dashboard.

| Sauyi                 | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                               | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                           |
| --------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`       | Ma'ajiyar lamba a tsarin `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`       | GitHub Personal Access Token mai izinin `issues:write`.                                                                                                                          |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(ba a saita ba)_ | tantance matsaloli / mataimakan wakilin cloud | Alamar samun damar GitHub ta gama-gari da ake amfani da ita a matsayin madadin `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN`, kuma mataimakan wakilin cloud a `src/lib/cloudAgent/*` suna amfani da ita. |

## Yanayin Turawa

Don jagorar SRE na backend ɗin relay (halayen ts/bifrost/auto, wurin sanya 9router idan aka kwatanta da CLIProxyAPI, da dabarar madadin don yawan zirga-zirga), duba [Dabarar Backend na Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Ƙaramin Muhallin Ci-gaban Gida

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Samarwa da Docker

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Muhalli Mara Haɗin Intanet / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS tare da Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

## 24. Skills Sandbox (v3.8.0+)

Iyakoki da saitunan tsaro da ake amfani da su lokacin da tsarin Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) ke aiwatar da ayyukan atomatik da masu amfani suka ayyana a cikin muhalli mai keɓewa.

| Sauyi                             | Tsoho                                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Iyakar lokacin agogo ga kowace aiwatarwar lambar skill a cikin sandbox. Iyaka ce tabbatacciya; ana kashe duk abin da ya wuce ta.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(yana komawa ga `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Iyakar lokacin tsara aiwatar da skill a babban mataki. Saita shi sama da `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` don ba da damar jerin ayyuka masu matakai da yawa.                                                                                                                                |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da skill zai iya karantawa daga kowane fayil guda ɗaya a cikin sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da ake ɗauka daga kowace amsar HTTP guda ɗaya a cikin skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tabbatacciyar iyaka ga haruffan stdout/stderr da ake dawo da su daga kiran sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Saita `1`/`true` don ba da damar sadarwar fita daga cikin sandbox. Ta tsohuwa tana kasancewa **keɓantacciya** saboda tsaro.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(babu komai)_                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Jerin container images da aka ware da waƙafi waɗanda aka amince da su don aiwatarwa a sandbox. Idan babu komai, ana amfani da tsohon ginannen zaɓi kawai.                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(tsohon ginannen zaɓi)_                       | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Container image da ake amfani da shi lokacin ƙirƙirar sandbox mai dogaro da Docker. Sauya shi don ɗaure amfani da keɓaɓɓen base image mai ƙarfafa tsaro.                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                         | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Container runtime don keɓe skills: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` yana zaɓar runtime mafi dacewa da aka shigar bisa OS na host (Apple Container/OrbStack a macOS, WSL Container a Windows, Podman a Linux), sannan ya koma Docker idan babu. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Kunna `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` yana buɗe hanyar fitar bayanai daga kowace lambar skill. Haɗa shi da `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` da tsauraran manufofin `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy a turawar da ake rabawa.

## 25. Ƙayyadaddun Amfani na Mai Bayarwa, Tunnels, Ajiyar Bayanai & Sauran Runtime

Endpoints na ƙayyadaddun amfani na masu bayarwa, tunnels na hanyar sadarwa (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), rukunin egress na 1Proxy, ajiyar bayanan database da ƙananan overrides na kowane fasali waɗanda layer na executor ko scripts ke ambata.

| Variable                                       | Default                                                                     | Source File                                                               | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Connection string na Redis don backend na rate limiter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(host na production)_                                                      | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Sauya host da ake amfani da shi wajen ɗauko ƙayyadaddun amfani na coding-plan na Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | an samo shi daga host                                                       | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Cikakken override na URL na ƙayyadaddun amfani don Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Cookie na zaman console don gateway na ƙayyadaddun amfani na Token Plan na mutum na Qwen Cloud / Model Studio (inference API key ba zai iya karanta shi ba). Kwafi dukkan request header na `Cookie` — yana ɗauke da `login_qwencloud_ticket` — daga kowane kiran `api.json` zuwa `cs-data.qwencloud.com` a home.qwencloud.com › Billing › Subscription (F12 › Network). Yana da muhimmancin tsaro kuma ya keɓanta ga session; an fi son field na Dashboard na `qwenCloudCookie` na kowane connection.                                                               |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Override na hannu na `sec_token` don gateway na console na Token Plan. Yana da muhimmancin tsaro; idan ba a saita shi ba, fetcher zai samo shi daga HTML na dashboard ta amfani da cookie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Override na host na gateway don fetcher na ƙayyadaddun amfani na Token Plan na mutum (misali, `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` don console na Model Studio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_DASHBOARD_URL`                | `https://home.qwencloud.com/`                                               | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | URL na dashboard da ake amfani da shi don samo `sec_token` daga HTML da aka shiga cikinsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_VISION_FE_PATH`             | `/costing-balance/free-quota-image-video`                                   | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Override na path na front-end na console don ɗauko ƙayyadaddun amfani na kyauta na vision/media na Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_MULTIMODAL_FE_PATH`         | `/costing-balance/free-quota-multimodal`                                    | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Sauya hanyar front-end ta Console don ɗauko ƙason kyauta na multimodal na Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_AUDIO_FE_PATH`              | `/costing-balance/free-quota-audio`                                         | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Sauya hanyar front-end ta Console don ɗauko ƙason kyauta na sauti na Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_ALLOWLIST_PATH`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierAllowlist.ts`                           | Hanya ta zaɓi zuwa fayil ɗin JSON na cikin gida da zai maye gurbin jerin izinin samfurorin rubutu na tsarin kyauta na Alibaba da ke ciki. Idan ba a samu ba, zai koma `$DATA_DIR/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`, sannan `config/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CONTEXT_RESERVE_TOKENS`                       | `1024`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Tokens da aka keɓe don fitowar kammalawa yayin lissafa iyakokin prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CONTEXT_KEEP_LATEST_IMAGES`                   | `2`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Adadin sabbin hotunan inline da za a riƙe yayin cire tsofaffin don su dace da tagar context (#8560).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MODEL_ALIAS_COMPAT_ENABLED`                   | an kunna                                                                    | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                                              | Kunna ko kashe tsohon tsarin dacewar laƙabin model da tsofaffin clients ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_EMERGENCY_FALLBACK`                 | an kunna                                                                    | `open-sse/services/emergencyFallback.ts`                                  | Saita `false` (ko `0`) don kashe hanyar madadin gaggawa lokacin da kasafin ya ƙare, wadda ke sake tura buƙatun da suka gaza zuwa model na kyauta `nvidia`/`openai/gpt-oss-120b`. Tsarin fifiko mai aiki shi ne sauyin Feature Flags DB > env var > tsohon ƙima; idan babu shi, service ɗin zai koma ga ainihin ƙimar env.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `COMMAND_CODE_CALLBACK_PORT`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `src/app/api/providers/command-code/auth/shared.ts`                       | Port na cikin gida da ake amfani da shi don callbacks irin na OAuth daga Command Code CLI helper.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `COMMAND_CODE_VERSION`                         | `0.33.2`                                                                    | `open-sse/executors/commandCode.ts`                                       | Ƙimar da ake aikawa a matsayin header na `x-command-code-version` zuwa Command Code upstream. Sauya ta don ɗaga sigar CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COMMANDCODE_API_URL`                          | `https://api.commandcode.ai`                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/command-code.ts`                                 | Tushen URL na Command Code usage/quota upstream da telemetry na mai ɗauko ƙason smartphone ke amfani da shi. Sauya shi don API na Command Code mai masaukin kansa ko na daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MITM_LOCAL_PORT`                              | `443`                                                                       | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Tashar haɗin gida don wakilin gyara kuskuren MITM.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MITM_DISABLE_TLS_VERIFY`                      | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Saita `1` don kashe tabbatar da TLS na uwar garken sama (don haɓakawa kawai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MITM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | `60000`                                                                     | `src/mitm/socketTimeouts.ts`, `src/mitm/server.cjs`                       | Wa'adin jiran soket marar aiki (ms) don haɗin da ake wakilta; ana katse soket marasa aiki da suka wuce wannan lokaci don guje wa barin hanyoyin sadarwa a rabin buɗe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MITM_VERBOSE`                                 | `1`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`, `src/mitm/_internal/bypass.cjs`                    | Yawan bayanan rajistar shawarar zaɓin hanya: `0` yana hana rajista, ƙimomi mafi girma suna rajistar ƙarin shawarwarin ketarewa/zaɓin hanya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MITM_ROOT_CA_ENABLED`                         | `false`                                                                     | `src/mitm/manager.ts`                                                     | Saita `true` don shiga tsarin tushen CA + takardar shaida ta kowane mai masaukin baki (#6684). Sabbin shigarwa suna samun sa ta atomatik; shigarwar da ke da tsohuwar amintacciyar takardar shaida suna ci gaba da amfani da tsohuwar takardar shaida mai tsayayyen SAN sai an zaɓi shiga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Tantancewa/iyakance ƙima/kariyar saka bayanai/jerin izini koyaushe suna fara aiki a hanyar Next. Martani suna ɗauke da `X-Routing-Backend` / `X-Routing-Fallback` / `X-Routing-Fallback-Reason`. |

| `OMNIROUTE_BIFROST_FAILURE_COOLDOWN_MS`        | `5000`                                                                      | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrostCooldown.ts`                | Lokacin jira (ms) bayan tsallakewar Bifrost sidecar ta gaza a yanayin `auto` kafin relay ya sake ƙoƙarin sidecar; yana tura kai tsaye zuwa hanyar TS muddin lokacin jiran bai ƙare ba, sannan ya sake gwadawa. `0` yana kashe wannan. Yana aiki ne kawai idan `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND=auto`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`                           | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Hanyar zuwa takardar shaidar TLS ta PEM don gudanar da `omniroute serve` ta HTTPS (daidai da `--tls-cert`). Dole ne a haɗa ta da `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`; sannan uwar garken mai zaman kanta za ta ƙare TLS a kan listener ɗin nan ɗin (`wss://` yana aiki ba tare da canji ba). Ba a saita ba → HTTP na yau da kullum. Samar da ɗaya kawai daga cert/key, ko hanyar da ba za a iya karantawa ba, yana rubuta gargaɗi kuma ya ci gaba da amfani da HTTP.                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Hanyar zuwa maɓallin sirri na TLS na PEM don HTTPS na `omniroute serve` (daidai da `--tls-key`). Dole ne a haɗa shi da `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`. Duba `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED`            | `0`                                                                         | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Babban maɓalli na hanyoyin `/api/local/*`. Idan ba a saita shi ba ko kuma yana `0`, duk hanyoyin `/api/local/*` suna mayar da 503 a production. Dole ne ya zama `1` a turawar da ba ta loopback ba domin kunna mai ƙaddamar da Redis da makamantan masu fara sabis na gida da dannawa 1. Ƙarin kariya tare da rabe-raben kariyar hanya na `isLocalOnlyPath()` (`LOCAL_ONLY_API_PREFIXES` a cikin `src/server/authz/routeGuard.ts`).                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_TOKEN`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Bearer token ga masu kiran `/api/local/*` waɗanda ba sa kan loopback (misali, manhajar desktop). Idan an saita shi, buƙatun daga IP da ba na loopback ba dole ne su ƙunshi `Authorization: Bearer <token>`. Ana buƙatarsa idan `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED=1` a turawar da ba ta loopback ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_HOST_PORT`                    | `6379`                                                                      | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Host port na mai ƙaddamar da Redis da dannawa 1. ### Mai Tsara Dumama na Claude

Dumama da cron ke sarrafawa don haɗin Anthropic OAuth da aka zaɓa, ta yadda za a buɗe tagar iyakar amfani ta sa’o’i 5 ta hanyar ƙaramin buƙata da aka tsara maimakon buƙata ta ainihi ta farko (#8848). Mai tsara aikin yana a kashe sai idan `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` yana da ƙimar gaskiya **kuma** an yi wa haɗin alama a cikin `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; jerin haɗi mara komai yana nufin ba za a dumama komai ba ko da an kunna canjin muhalli.

| Canji                          | Tsoho                       | Fayil ɗin Tushe              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Babban maɓallin kunna mai tsara dumama. Yana karɓar `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (ba ya kula da bambancin manyan da ƙananan haruffa, kuma ana cire sarari). Duk wata ƙima dabam, ko rashin saita shi, yana barin mai tsara aikin a kashe. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Bayanin cron mai filaye biyar don zagayen dumama, wanda ake kimantawa a `America/Los_Angeles` (yankin lokacin sake saiti na Anthropic) ba tare da la’akari da agogon uwar garken ba.                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Adadin haɗin da ake dumamawa a lokaci guda a kowane zagaye. Ana iyakance shi zuwa `1`-`10`; ƙimar da ba lamba ba tana komawa zuwa `3`.                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Samfurin da ake amfani da shi don buƙatar dumama. Kada a sauya sai idan tsohon zaɓin ba ya samuwa a shirinka; zaɓi samfurin mafi arha wanda har yanzu zai buɗe tagar.                                                              |

### Zaman VNC na Shiga ta Burauza da Laƙabin Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC da aka sanya cikin kwantena wanda ake amfani da shi don karɓar bayanan shiga ta burauza kai-tsaye (`/api/vnc-session`), tare da tsohon laƙabin `DATA_DIR`. Dukansu na zaɓi ne — tsoffin saitunan VNC suna nufin hoton `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` da aka haɗa, kuma ana sauya su ne kawai don hoton kwantena na musamman, tashoshin sadarwa, ko daidaita zagayen rayuwa.

| Sauyi                                 | Tsoho                                                                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Alamar hoton Docker don kwantenar shiga ta Chromium+VNC. Gina `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ko nuna wannan zuwa wani hoto na musamman.                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Fayil ɗin binary na tsarin tafiyar da kwantena da ake amfani da shi don ƙaddamar da kwantenar VNC (misali, saita shi zuwa `podman`).                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar VNC/noVNC da aka buɗe a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar Chrome DevTools Protocol a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar kundin bayanan martabar Chromium a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin da ke kan na'urar mai masauki wanda ke ɗauke da bayanan martabar shiga ta burauza da aka adana.                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (minti 10)                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin rashin aiki (ms) kafin a cire zaman VNC mara aiki.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (minti 30)                                                     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iyakar ƙarshe (ms) ta tsawon lokacin zaman VNC guda ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin adadin zaman VNC da za su iya gudana lokaci guda.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin jira (ms) kafin burauzar da ke cikin kwantenar ta kasance a shirye don CDP.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin lokaci (ms) don tattara zaman/kukis da aka kama bayan an kammala shiga.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin tutocin layin umarni da ake miƙawa ga Chromium da ke cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Keɓaɓɓiyar hanyar sadarwar Docker da kwantenar shiga ta VNC ke shiga (#12571) maimakon gadar tsohuwa, don haka kwantenoni 'yan'uwa ba za su iya isa tashar gadar CDP ɗinta ba.                                                        |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                        | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Tsohon suna na madadin** don `DATA_DIR`, wanda ake dubawa ne kawai bayan an ga cewa ba a saita `DATA_DIR` da `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` ba. Yana gano ma'ajiyar zaman zaren yanar gizon Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Gwaji & Kayan Aikin E2E

Ana amfani da su ta `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, da `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. A bar duk
ƙimomin da ke ƙasa ba tare da saita su ba a turawar samarwa.

| Sauyi                                  | Tsoho                             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Yanayin fara E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) don mai gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | yana komawa ga `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kalmar sirrin mai gudanarwa da ake shigarwa cikin muhallin Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe binciken lafiyar gida na lokaci-lokaci yayin gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe madaukin binciken lafiyar token na OAuth yayin gwaje-gwaje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Masu samarwa da aka raba da wakafi waɗanda aka cire daga binciken sabunta token na rigakafi (misali `codex,openai`). Madadin da aka keɓance na kashe binciken lafiya gaba ɗaya — masu samarwa masu gajeren TTL suna ci gaba da sabuntawa yayin da masu samarwa na cascade ke kasancewa masu amsawa kawai. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Dakatar da hayaniyar bayanan binciken lafiya a stdout na Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Tsallake ginin samarwa na Next.js kafin Playwright ya fara (ingantawa ta CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                               | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Tsallake hook ɗin cire OmniRoute (CI na amfani da shi don kiyaye `node_modules` yadda yake).                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                          | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Lokacin jira (ms) har sai uwar garken ya kasance lafiya kafin gudanar da gwaje-gwajen tsarin muhalli/yarjejeniya.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`    | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL da kayan aikin gwajin smoke na Electron ke tsammanin manhajar da aka shirya za ta samar.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                           | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Jimillar iyakar lokaci (ms) kafin kayan aikin gwajin smoke ya daina jira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tagar jira (ms) bayan shafin ya loda.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(ta atomatik)_                   | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Takamaiman hanyar zuwa executable na Electron da aka shirya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Kundin bayanai don gudanar da gwajin smoke na Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don adana kundin adireshin bayanan gwajin smoke bayan an gama gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don watsa rajistocin Electron zuwa stdout yayin gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: sake ƙaddamarwa ta amfani da kundin adireshin bayanai ɗaya, sannan a tabbatar cewa ƙaddamarwa ta biyu ta zaɓi direban SQLite na asali.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(binciken PATH)_                 | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Sauya hanyar binary ta Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### Bututun fassarar takardu

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` (umarnin `npm run i18n:run`).
Dukkan masu canji biyar ba a saita su ta tsohuwa ba — saita su a cikin `.env` kawai a kan na'urorin
da ya kamata su iya gudanar da mai fassara takardun.

| Mai canji                           | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                    | Bayani                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`     | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | URL na tushe mai dacewa da OpenAI don tsarin bayan fassara.                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`     | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Alamar Bearer don tsarin bayan fassara (ba a taɓa rubuta ta a rajista ba).         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`       | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | ID na samfurin, misali `gpt-4o-mini` ko `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `60000`           | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Iyakar lokacin kowace buƙata a cikin milliseƙan.                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY` | `4`               | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Buƙatun fassara masu gudana a layi ɗaya yayin aiki kan fayiloli / locales da yawa. |
